+/**
+ * Quantity limits for new/edited prescriptions, after lossless shape parsing.
+ * Separate from legacy normalization: old commands could persist more than
+ * eight steps, which reads, migration and replay must preserve.
+ */
+export function sessionStructureWithinWriteLimits(structure: MobileSessionStructure): boolean {
+  return structure.blocks.length <= MAX_BLOCKS && structure.blocks.every(block =>
+    Number.isInteger(block.iterations) && block.iterations >= 1 && block.iterations <= MAX_BLOCK_ITERATIONS &&
+    block.steps.length >= 1 && block.steps.length <= MAX_STEPS_PER_BLOCK);
+}

+/** Stable digest over every fact that can appear in a candidate page. */
+export function executionCandidateFactsDigest(facts: readonly ExecutionFact[], range: ExecutionRange): string {
+  return digest({ contract: "rai-training-plan-execution-candidates-v1",
+    rangeStart: range.rangeStart, rangeEnd: range.rangeEnd,
+    facts: factsInRange(facts, range).map(fact => ({
+      trainingSessionId: fact.trainingSessionId, startedAt: fact.startedAt, endedAt: fact.endedAt,
+      distanceMeters: fact.distanceMeters, durationSeconds: fact.durationSeconds,
+      sourceFactDigest: fact.sourceFactDigest
+    })) });
+}
+
+/**
+ * A cursor is a continuation token, not a capability token: it authorises no
+ * write and is only accepted for the exact contract, scope, range and fact set
+ * that produced it. Clients must restart from page 1 when any of those change.
+ */
+export class ExecutionCursorError extends PlanOperationError {
+  constructor(message = "执行候选游标非法，请重新读取。") {
+    super("training_session_command_invalid", message, 422);
+    this.name = "ExecutionCursorError";
+  }
+}
+
+export interface ExecutionCursorContext {
+  /** Canonical scope key supplied by the caller (same string used for control rows). */
+  scope?: string | null;
+  range: ExecutionRange;
+  /** Digest of the full candidate fact set in `range`, not just the current page. */
+  factsDigest: string;
+}
+

-export function encodeExecutionCursor(cursor: ExecutionCursor): string {

+const EXECUTION_CURSOR_CONTRACT = "rai-training-plan-execution-cursor-v1";
+const EXECUTION_CURSOR_VERSION = TRAINING_PLAN_CLOSED_LOOP_CONTRACT.executionCommands.version;
+const CURSOR_BASE64URL = /^[A-Za-z0-9_-]+$/;
+
+/** Encode a continuation token bound to the exact query that issued it. */
+export function encodeExecutionCursor(cursor: ExecutionCursor, context: ExecutionCursorContext): string {
+  if (typeof context.scope !== "string" || context.scope.length === 0) {
+    throw new ExecutionCursorError("候选游标缺少 scope 上下文，请重新读取候选。");
+  }
+  return Buffer.from(JSON.stringify({
+    contract: EXECUTION_CURSOR_CONTRACT,
+    version: EXECUTION_CURSOR_VERSION,
+    scope: context.scope,
+    rangeStart: context.range.rangeStart,
+    rangeEnd: context.range.rangeEnd,
+    factsDigest: context.factsDigest,
+    startedAt: cursor.startedAt,
+    trainingSessionId: cursor.trainingSessionId
+  }), "utf8").toString("base64url");
 }
 
-export function parseExecutionCursor(value: string): ExecutionCursor {
+/**
+ * Decode and validate a cursor. With `expected`, the contract/scope/range/fact
+ * binding is enforced; without it only structural validity and the cursor
+ * contract version are checked (useful for diagnostics/tests).
+ */
+export function parseExecutionCursor(value: string, expected?: ExecutionCursorContext): ExecutionCursor {
+  let payload: Record<string, unknown>;
   try {
-    const parsed: unknown = JSON.parse(Buffer.from(value, "base64url").toString("utf8"));
-    if (!Array.isArray(parsed) || parsed.length !== 2 || typeof parsed[0] !== "string" || typeof parsed[1] !== "string" ||
-        !Number.isFinite(Date.parse(parsed[0])) || !parsed[1]) throw new Error("shape");

+    if (typeof value !== "string" || value.length === 0 || value.length > 4_096 || !CURSOR_BASE64URL.test(value)) {
+      throw new Error("base64url");
+    }
+    const bytes = Buffer.from(value, "base64url");
+    if (bytes.length === 0 || bytes.toString("base64url") !== value) throw new Error("base64url");
+    const parsed: unknown = JSON.parse(bytes.toString("utf8"));
+    if (parsed === null || typeof parsed !== "object" || Array.isArray(parsed)) throw new Error("shape");
+    payload = parsed as Record<string, unknown>;
   } catch {
-    throw new Error("execution cursor is invalid");
+    throw new ExecutionCursorError("执行候选游标格式非法，请重新读取。");
+  }
+  if (payload.contract !== EXECUTION_CURSOR_CONTRACT || payload.version !== EXECUTION_CURSOR_VERSION ||
+      typeof payload.scope !== "string" || payload.scope.length === 0 ||
+      typeof payload.rangeStart !== "string" || !isValidLocalDate(payload.rangeStart) ||
+      typeof payload.rangeEnd !== "string" || !isValidLocalDate(payload.rangeEnd) ||
+      typeof payload.factsDigest !== "string" || !/^[a-f0-9]{64}$/.test(payload.factsDigest) ||
+      typeof payload.startedAt !== "string" || !Number.isFinite(Date.parse(payload.startedAt)) ||
+      typeof payload.trainingSessionId !== "string" || payload.trainingSessionId.length === 0) {
+    throw new ExecutionCursorError("执行候选游标格式非法，请重新读取。");
+  }
+  if (expected !== undefined) {
+    if (payload.scope !== expected.scope || payload.rangeStart !== expected.range.rangeStart ||
+        payload.rangeEnd !== expected.range.rangeEnd || payload.factsDigest !== expected.factsDigest) {
+      throw new ExecutionCursorError("执行候选游标与当前 scope、范围或事实不匹配，请重新读取候选。");
+    }
   }
+  return { startedAt: payload.startedAt, trainingSessionId: payload.trainingSessionId };
 }

+/** Only supplied content is new. Metadata operations retain historical steps. */
+function assertDraftStructureLimits(draft: TrainingPlanSessionDraft): void {
+  const structure = draft.action === "add" ? draft.prescription.structure
+    : draft.action === "edit" ? draft.patch.structure : null;
+  if (structure && !sessionStructureWithinWriteLimits(structure)) invalid("训练结构最多8个区块，每区块最多8步、20次重复。");
+}

+/**
+ * Parse before AJV can coerce input. Defer schema errors to the handler so an
+ * already committed historical operation replays before new-write validation.
+ */
+export function trainingSessionCommandRouteOptions(submit: boolean) {
+  return {
+    schema: { body: submit ? trainingSessionSubmitBodySchema : trainingSessionPreviewBodySchema },
+    attachValidation: true,
+    preValidation: async (request: FastifyRequest, reply: FastifyReply) => {
+      reply.header("Cache-Control", "no-store");
+      try { parseTrainingSessionRequest(request.body, submit); }
+      catch (error) {
+        if (error instanceof PlanOperationError) return reply.code(error.statusCode).send({ code: error.code, message: error.message });
+        throw error;
+      }
+    }
+  };
+}

+  lockedAt: string | null;
+  availableAt: string | null;
+}
+
+// Inspect raw historical payloads before a strict parser rejects them. Only
+// dates and parseable timestamps leave this boundary, never a prescription.
+export function trainingPlanLockedRestConflicts(raw: unknown): TrainingPlanLockedRestConflict[] {
+  if (!sessionRecord(raw) || !sessionRecord(raw.lockedSession)) return [];
+  const lock = raw.lockedSession, date = lock.localDate;
+  if (typeof date !== "string" || !isValidLocalDate(date)) return [];
+  const lockedPrescription = sessionRecord(lock.session) ? lock.session : null;
+  const lockedKind = MOBILE_SESSION_KINDS.find(kind => kind === lockedPrescription?.kind) ?? null;
+  const instant = (value: unknown): string | null => typeof value === "string" &&
+    /^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}T/.test(value) && Number.isFinite(Date.parse(value)) ? new Date(value).toISOString() : null;
+  const conflicts: TrainingPlanLockedRestConflict[] = [];
+  if (lockedKind !== "rest" && Array.isArray(raw.weekPlan) && raw.weekPlan.some(day => sessionRecord(day) && day.localDate === date && day.kind === "rest")) {
+    conflicts.push({ localDate: date, lockedKind, kind: "locked_session_week_plan_rest", lockedAt: instant(lock.lockedAt), availableAt: null });
+  }
+  if (Array.isArray(raw.suppressions)) for (const value of raw.suppressions) {
+    if (sessionRecord(value) && value.localDate === date && value.reason === "explicit_rest") {
+      conflicts.push({ localDate: date, lockedKind, kind: "locked_session_explicit_rest", lockedAt: instant(lock.lockedAt), availableAt: instant(value.availableAt) });
+    }
+  }
+  return conflicts;
+}
+
+export function assertTrainingPlanLockedRestInvariant(raw: unknown): void {
+  // Legacy can lock rest itself. Its matching rest materialization is one intent,
+  // not a running prescription colliding with rest. explicit_rest remains strict.
+  const conflicts = trainingPlanLockedRestConflicts(raw);
+  if (conflicts.length > 0) {
+    const kind = conflicts.some(value => value.kind === "locked_session_explicit_rest") ? "explicit rest" : "weekPlan rest";
+    throw new TrainingPlanSessionStateError(
+      `training_plan_locked_rest_conflict: ${kind} and locked session share a date: ${conflicts[0]!.localDate}`);
+  }
+}
 export function sessionKeys(value: Record<string, unknown>, keys: readonly string[], path: string): void {
   for (const key of Object.keys(value)) if (!keys.includes(key)) fail(`${path}: unknown field ${key}`);
 }

   const instance = sessionId(raw.planInstanceId, "planInstanceId");
   if (!Array.isArray(raw.sessions) || !Array.isArray(raw.suppressions)) return fail("sessions/suppressions arrays required");

+++ b/apps/api/src/training-plan-state-audit.ts

+import { DatabaseSync } from "node:sqlite";
+import { statSync } from "node:fs";
+import { parseTrainingPlanScopeKey, parseTrainingPlanState } from "./training-plan-store.js";
+import { sessionRecord, trainingPlanLockedRestConflicts, type TrainingPlanLockedRestConflict } from "./training-plan-session-state.js";
+
+interface Location { source: string; scope: string; revision: number; schemaVersion: number | null }
+export interface TrainingPlanStateAuditReport {
+  ok: boolean;
+  complete: boolean;
+  readOnly: true;
+  snapshots: number;
+  scopes: number;
+  affectedScopes: number | null;
+  conflicts: Array<Location & TrainingPlanLockedRestConflict>;
+  issues: Array<Partial<Location> & { code: string }>;
+}
+
+/** Explicit offline copy only. No runtime bootstrap, migrations or environment defaults. */
+export function auditTrainingPlanStateCopy(path: string): TrainingPlanStateAuditReport {
+  const report: TrainingPlanStateAuditReport = { ok: false, complete: false, readOnly: true, snapshots: 0, scopes: 0,
+    affectedScopes: null, conflicts: [], issues: [] };
+  let database: DatabaseSync | undefined;
+  try {
+    try { if (!statSync(path).isFile()) throw new Error(); }
+    catch { report.issues.push({ code: "control_copy_missing_or_not_file" }); return report; }
+    try { database = new DatabaseSync(path, { readOnly: true }); }
+    catch { report.issues.push({ code: "control_copy_unavailable" }); return report; }
+    database.exec("BEGIN");
+    const sources = ["mobile_training_state", "mobile_training_state_history"];
+    for (const source of sources) {
+      const columns = database.prepare(`PRAGMA table_info(${source})`).all().map(row => row.name);
+      if (!["scope", "revision", "payload_json"].every(name => columns.includes(name))) {
+        report.issues.push({ source, code: "state_table_schema_mismatch" });
+      }
+    }
+    if (report.issues.length > 0) return report;
+    const scopes = new Set<string>(), affected = new Set<string>();
+    for (const source of sources) {
+      // Iterate snapshots instead of retaining historical health-bearing payloads.
+      for (const row of database.prepare(`SELECT scope,revision,payload_json FROM ${source} ORDER BY scope,revision`).iterate()) {
+        report.snapshots++;
+        const scope = String(row.scope), revision = Number(row.revision);
+        const location: Location = { source, scope, revision, schemaVersion: null };
+        scopes.add(scope);
+        try { parseTrainingPlanScopeKey(scope); }
+        catch { report.issues.push({ ...location, code: "invalid_scope" }); }
+        if (!Number.isSafeInteger(revision) || revision < 0) report.issues.push({ ...location, code: "invalid_revision" });
+        let raw: unknown;
+        try { raw = JSON.parse(String(row.payload_json)); }
+        catch { report.issues.push({ ...location, code: "invalid_json" }); continue; }
+        location.schemaVersion = sessionRecord(raw) && typeof raw.schemaVersion === "number" ? raw.schemaVersion : null;
+        const conflicts = trainingPlanLockedRestConflicts(raw);
+        if (conflicts.length > 0) {
+          affected.add(scope);
+          report.conflicts.push(...conflicts.map(conflict => ({ ...location, ...conflict })));
+        }
+        if (location.schemaVersion !== 1 && location.schemaVersion !== 2) {
+          report.issues.push({ ...location, code: "unsupported_state_schema" });
+        } else if (conflicts.length === 0) {
+          try { parseTrainingPlanState(raw); }
+          catch { report.issues.push({ ...location, code: "invalid_state" }); }
+        }
+      }
+    }
+    report.scopes = scopes.size;
+    report.complete = report.issues.length === 0;
+    report.affectedScopes = report.complete ? affected.size : null;
+    report.ok = report.complete && report.conflicts.length === 0;
+    return report;
+  } catch {
+    // SQLite/parser error strings can contain payload fragments or local paths.
+    report.issues.push({ code: "control_copy_read_failed" });
+    return report;
+  } finally {
+    if (database) { if (database.isTransaction) database.exec("ROLLBACK"); database.close(); }
+  }
+}

+test("B06: candidate facts retain real runs with null or zero dose for completed + unknown assessment", async () => {
+  const root = mkdtempSync(join(tmpdir(), "rai-execution-facts-dose-"));
+  const databasePath = join(root, "rai.sqlite");
+  const store = await seed(databasePath);
+  store.close();
+  const writer = new DatabaseSync(databasePath);
+  try {
+    // Three real runs with unknown/zero dose: null distance, zero distance and
+    // zero duration. None may disappear from candidate reads. (`duration_seconds`
+    // is NOT NULL in the current main schema, but the read type keeps null for
+    // forward compatibility; the mapping no longer filters either value.)
+    writer.prepare("UPDATE training_sessions SET anchor_distance_km=NULL WHERE id='api_session_6'").run();
+    writer.prepare("UPDATE training_sessions SET anchor_distance_km=0 WHERE id='api_session_5'").run();
+    writer.prepare("UPDATE workouts SET duration_seconds=0 WHERE id='api_workout_7'").run();
+  } finally {
+    writer.close();
+  }
+  const read = new RaiReadStore(databasePath);
+  try {
+    const once = read.executionCandidateFacts("2025-01-01", "2025-01-31");
+    const twice = read.executionCandidateFacts("2025-01-01", "2025-01-31");
+    assert.equal(once.length, 8, "dose null/zero is evidence, not an eligibility filter");
+    assert.deepEqual(once, twice, "the candidate set and digests stay stable across reads");
+    const nullDistance = once.find(fact => fact.trainingSessionId === "api_session_6")!;
+    assert.equal(nullDistance.distanceMeters, null);
+    assert.equal(nullDistance.durationSeconds, 1800);
+    const zeroDistance = once.find(fact => fact.trainingSessionId === "api_session_5")!;
+    assert.equal(zeroDistance.distanceMeters, 0);
+    const zeroDuration = once.find(fact => fact.trainingSessionId === "api_session_7")!;
+    assert.equal(zeroDuration.durationSeconds, 0);
+    assert.ok(once.every(fact => /^[a-f0-9]{64}$/.test(fact.sourceFactDigest)));
+  } finally {
+    read.close();
+    rmSync(root, { recursive: true, force: true });
+  }
+});

+test("P1.2: superseded and cancelled courses cannot be confirmed, corrected, skipped or used for candidates", () => {
+  const f = setup();
+  try {
+    const row = f.row();
+    const state = structuredClone(f.state()) as TrainingPlanSessionState;
+    const template = structuredClone(state.sessions[0]!);
+    const old: TrainingPlanSession = { ...structuredClone(template), sessionId: "superseded-old", sessionRevision: 1,
+      prescription: { ...structuredClone(template.prescription), localDate: "2026-10-27" },
+      supersededBySessionId: "replacement-live", protection: "automatic", userOverride: null };
+    const replacement: TrainingPlanSession = { ...structuredClone(template), sessionId: "replacement-live",
+      sessionRevision: 1, prescription: { ...structuredClone(template.prescription), localDate: "2026-10-27" },
+      supersededBySessionId: null, protection: "automatic", userOverride: null };
+    const cancelled: TrainingPlanSession = { ...structuredClone(template), sessionId: "cancelled-course",
+      sessionRevision: 1, prescription: { ...structuredClone(template.prescription), localDate: "2026-10-26" },
+      lifecycle: "cancelled", protection: "automatic", userOverride: null };
+    state.sessions.push(old, replacement, cancelled);
+    state.weekPlan = [...state.weekPlan, replacement.prescription].sort((left, right) => left.localDate.localeCompare(right.localDate));
+    f.goals.withPublicationTransaction(() => {
+      const projected = f.goals.write({ weekPlan: state.weekPlan }, { preserveWeekPlan: true, sessionCommand: true });
+      f.goals.commitTrainingPlanCommandState({ scope: key, expectedRevision: row.revision,
+        state: { ...state, goalsRevision: projected.revision }, eventType: "fixture:superseded-and-cancelled" });
+    });
+    const planInstanceId = f.state().planInstanceId;
+    const expected = code("training_session_not_editable");
+    for (const id of ["superseded-old", "cancelled-course"]) {
+      assert.throws(() => f.service.candidates(id, { planInstanceId }), expected);
+      assert.throws(() => f.execute({ action: "confirm", trainingSessionId: "run-easy",
+        sourceFactDigest: f.facts()[0]!.sourceFactDigest }, id), expected);
+      assert.throws(() => f.execute({ action: "correct", trainingSessionId: "run-easy",
+        sourceFactDigest: f.facts()[0]!.sourceFactDigest }, id), expected);
+      assert.throws(() => f.execute({ action: "skip", reason: "stale client" }, id), expected);
+    }
+    assert.ok(f.service.candidates("replacement-live", { planInstanceId }).candidates.length > 0,
+      "the live replacement remains reachable");
+  } finally { f.close(); }
+});

+test("P2.1: correct always re-reads the live source; a deleted/corrected fact is source_changed, never no_change", () => {
+  const f = setup();
+  try {
+    const source = f.facts()[0]!;
+    f.execute({ action: "confirm", trainingSessionId: source.trainingSessionId, sourceFactDigest: source.sourceFactDigest });
+    f.setFacts(f.facts().filter(item => item.trainingSessionId !== source.trainingSessionId));
+    assert.throws(() => f.execute({ action: "correct", trainingSessionId: source.trainingSessionId,
+      sourceFactDigest: source.sourceFactDigest }), code("training_execution_source_changed"));
+    f.setFacts([{ ...source, distanceMeters: 9900, sourceFactDigest: digest({ correctedAfterNoop: true }) }]);
+    assert.throws(() => f.execute({ action: "correct", trainingSessionId: source.trainingSessionId,
+      sourceFactDigest: source.sourceFactDigest }), code("training_execution_source_changed"));
+    assert.equal(f.goals.trainingPlanExecution(key, fixture.initial.sessionId)!.execution.association, "confirmed");
+  } finally { f.close(); }
+});

+test("P2.2: reconcile re-reads facts in the transaction and skips a source that recovered before commit", () => {
+  const f = setup();
+  try {
+    const source = f.facts()[0]!;
+    f.execute({ action: "confirm", trainingSessionId: source.trainingSessionId, sourceFactDigest: source.sourceFactDigest });
+    const original = f.facts();
+    const corrected = original.map(item => item.trainingSessionId === source.trainingSessionId
+      ? { ...item, distanceMeters: 9900, sourceFactDigest: digest({ transientCorrection: true }) } : item);
+    let calls = 0;
+    const provider: ExecutionCommandFacts = {
+      fingerprint: () => "stable-facts",
+      candidates: () => { calls++; return calls === 1 ? corrected : original; },
+      workout: () => null
+    };
+    const service = new TrainingPlanExecutionCommandService(f.goals, () => scope, () => businessDate,
+      () => provider, () => Date.parse(nowInstant));
+    const before = businessSnapshot(f.db);
+    assert.equal(service.reconcileSourceChanges().changed, 0);
+    assert.deepEqual(businessSnapshot(f.db), before, "no-change reconciliation must not save an operation tombstone");
+    assert.ok(calls >= 2, "the transaction re-read the candidate instead of trusting the pre-scan");
+    const execution = f.goals.trainingPlanExecution(key, fixture.initial.sessionId)!.execution;
+    assert.equal(execution.association, "confirmed");
+    assert.equal(execution.executionRevision, 1);
+    assert.equal(f.goals.trainingPlanExecutionHistory(key, fixture.initial.sessionId).length, 1);
+  } finally { f.close(); }
+});

+test("P1.5: an edit previewed before the run but committed after it is not the pre-run prescription", () => {
+  const f = setup();
+  try {
+    const current = f.session();
+    const sessionService = new TrainingPlanSessionCommandService(f.goals, () => scope, () => businessDate,
+      () => ({ fingerprint: "session-facts", prescription: null, validate: () => {} }), () => f.now());
+    const beforeRun = "2026-10-27T23:59:00.000Z";
+    f.clock(Date.parse(beforeRun) - Date.parse(nowInstant));
+    const request = { contractVersion: 1 as const, operationScope: scope, expectedStateRevision: f.row().revision,
+      planInstanceId: f.state().planInstanceId, draft: { action: "edit" as const, sessionId: current.sessionId,
+        sessionRevision: current.sessionRevision, patch: { reason: "written after the run" } } };
+    const preview = sessionService.preview(request);
+    f.clock(105_000);
+    const submitted = sessionService.submit({ ...request, candidateId: preview.candidateId, operationId: randomUUID() });
+    assert.equal(submitted.session!.availableAt, "2026-10-28T00:00:45.000Z");
+    assert.ok(Date.parse(submitted.session!.availableAt) > Date.parse("2026-10-28T00:00:00.000Z"));
+    f.setFacts(f.facts().map(item => item.trainingSessionId === "run-easy"
+      ? { ...item, endedAt: "2026-10-28T00:00:30.000Z" } : item));
+    const confirmed = f.execute({ action: "confirm", trainingSessionId: "run-easy",
+      sourceFactDigest: f.facts()[0]!.sourceFactDigest });
+    assert.equal(confirmed.execution!.prescriptionSessionRevision, 1,
+      "the run freezes the revision that was actually published before it started");
+  } finally { f.close(); }
+});

+// I-01/P-03: real main-library facts and control publications use separate SQLite files.
+async function skipFixture() {
+  const f = setup();
+  const main = await RaiDatabase.open(join(f.root, "facts.sqlite"));
+  const reads = new ReadStoreManager(join(f.root, "facts.sqlite"));
+  const provider: ExecutionCommandFacts = {
+    fingerprint: () => reads.dataVersionFingerprint(),
+    candidates: (start, end) => reads.use(store => store.executionCandidateFacts(start, end)),
+    workout: () => null
+  };
+  const service = new TrainingPlanExecutionCommandService(f.goals, () => scope, () => businessDate, () => provider, () => f.now());
+  const control = (f.goals as unknown as { controlDatabase: DatabaseSync }).controlDatabase;
+  const addRun = (startedAt = "2026-10-28T00:00:00.000Z") => {
+    const endedAt = new Date(Date.parse(startedAt) + 1800_000).toISOString();
+    main.database.prepare(`INSERT INTO workouts(id,activity_type,source_name,source_category,source_version,device,
+      start_at,end_at,duration_seconds,indoor,metadata_json,raw_attributes_json,is_canonical)
+      VALUES ('skip-workout','HKWorkoutActivityTypeRunning','Fixture','apple_watch','1','Watch',?,?,1800,0,'{}','{}',1)`)
+      .run(startedAt, endedAt);
+    main.database.prepare(`INSERT INTO training_sessions(id,activity_type,anchor_start_at,anchor_end_at,
+      anchor_distance_km,canonical_workout_id,status) VALUES ('skip-run','HKWorkoutActivityTypeRunning',?,?,5,'skip-workout','active')`)
+      .run(startedAt, endedAt);
+  };
+  const snapshot = () => Object.fromEntries(["mobile_training_state", "mobile_training_state_history",
+    "mobile_training_session_history", "mobile_training_execution", "mobile_training_execution_history",
+    "mobile_plan_operations"].map(table => [table, f.db.prepare(`SELECT * FROM ${table} ORDER BY rowid`).all()]));
+  return { ...f, main, reads, provider, service, control, addRun, snapshot,
+    close: () => { reads.close(); main.close(); f.close(); } };
+}

+for (const point of ["candidate read", "operation receipt"] as const) {
+  test(`P-03: a real main-DB version change at ${point} rolls back the whole Skip publication`, async () => {
+    const f = await skipFixture();
+    try {
+      const before = f.snapshot();
+      const request = f.body({ action: "skip", reason: "race" });
+      if (point === "candidate read") {
+        const original = f.provider.candidates;
+        f.provider.candidates = (start, end) => { const rows = original(start, end); f.addRun(); return rows; };
+      } else {
+        // Runs after state/history and result insertion, immediately before the real COMMIT.
+        f.control.function("p03_arriving_fact", () => { f.addRun(); return 1; });
+        f.control.exec(`CREATE TRIGGER p03_arriving_fact AFTER INSERT ON mobile_plan_operations
+          BEGIN SELECT p03_arriving_fact(); END`);
+      }
+      assert.throws(() => f.service.submit(request), code("training_execution_source_changed"));
+      assert.deepEqual(f.snapshot(), before, "no skipped state or partial receipt survives the cross-store conflict");
+      assert.equal(f.reads.use(store => store.executionCandidateFacts("2026-10-26", "2026-10-30")).length, 1);
+    } finally { f.close(); }
+  });
+}

+test("P-03: empty-window Skip replays unchanged after facts arrive; a fresh duplicate Skip is rejected", async () => {
+  const f = await skipFixture();
+  try {
+    const request = f.body({ action: "skip", reason: "休息" });
+    const result = f.service.submit(request);
+    assert.equal(result.session!.lifecycle, "skipped");
+    const after = f.snapshot();
+    f.addRun();
+    f.provider.fingerprint = () => { throw new Error("replay must not read facts"); };
+    assert.deepEqual(f.service.submit(request), result);
+    assert.deepEqual(f.snapshot(), after);
+    assert.throws(() => f.service.submit(f.body({ action: "skip", reason: "again" })), code("training_session_not_editable"));
+    assert.deepEqual(f.snapshot(), after);

+for (const failure of ["state", "receipt", "commit"] as const) {
+  test(`P-03: SQLite ${failure} failure leaves no Skip or partial operation`, async () => {
+    const f = await skipFixture();
+    try {
+      const request = f.body({ action: "skip", reason: "rollback" });
+      const before = f.snapshot();
+      if (failure === "commit") {
+        f.control.exec(`PRAGMA foreign_keys=ON;
+          CREATE TABLE p03_parent(id TEXT PRIMARY KEY);
+          CREATE TABLE p03_fault(ref TEXT REFERENCES p03_parent(id) DEFERRABLE INITIALLY DEFERRED);
+          CREATE TRIGGER p03_fault AFTER INSERT ON mobile_plan_operations BEGIN INSERT INTO p03_fault VALUES ('missing'); END`);
+      } else {
+        const table = failure === "state" ? "mobile_training_state" : "mobile_plan_operations";
+        const event = failure === "state" ? "UPDATE" : "INSERT";
+        f.control.exec(`CREATE TRIGGER p03_fault BEFORE ${event} ON ${table} BEGIN SELECT RAISE(ABORT,'P03 injected failure'); END`);
+      }
+      assert.throws(() => f.service.submit(request), failure === "commit" ? /FOREIGN KEY/ : /P03 injected failure/);
+      assert.deepEqual(f.snapshot(), before);
+    } finally { f.close(); }
+  });

+test("P-03: Skip/Confirm share the fact-writer lease; replay bypasses admission and the lease", async () => {
+  const f = await skipFixture();
+  try {
+    const mutations = new MutationCoordinator(), admission = new TaskAdmissionScheduler(mutations);
+    const worker = admission.owner("fact-writer");
+    const releaseWorker = worker.acquire()!;
+    const request = f.body({ action: "skip", reason: "race" });
+    let leases = 0, admitted = true;
+    const handlers = createTrainingExecutionCommandHandlers(f.service, () => { assert.ok(admitted); }, async fn => {
+      leases++;
+      const release = await mutations.acquireWhenAvailable("execution");
+      try { assert.equal(worker.acquire(), null, "fact writer cannot change either DB through the coordinated lane"); return await fn(); }
+      finally { release(); worker.withdraw(); }
+    });
+    const req = (body: TrainingPlanExecutionCommandRequest) => ({ body, log: { error() {} } }) as unknown as FastifyRequest;
+    const reply = () => ({ statusCode: 200, body: null as unknown, header() { return this; },
+      code(value: number) { this.statusCode = value; return this; }, send(value: unknown) { this.body = value; return value; } });
+    const skipReply = reply();
+    const pendingSkip = handlers.submit(req(request), skipReply as unknown as FastifyReply);
+    f.addRun(); releaseWorker();
+    const fact = f.provider.candidates("2026-10-26", "2026-10-30")[0]!;
+    const confirmReply = reply();
+    const pendingConfirm = handlers.submit(req(f.body({ action: "confirm", trainingSessionId: fact.trainingSessionId,
+      sourceFactDigest: fact.sourceFactDigest })), confirmReply as unknown as FastifyReply);
+    await Promise.all([pendingSkip, pendingConfirm]);
+    assert.equal(leases, 2);
+    assert.equal(confirmReply.statusCode, 200);
+    assert.equal(skipReply.statusCode, 409);
+    assert.ok(["training_execution_candidates_present", "training_state_revision_conflict"].includes((skipReply.body as {code:string}).code));
+    assert.equal(f.session().lifecycle, "partial", "the real 5 km run confirms identity but falls short of the 8 km prescription");
+    const confirmed = f.goals.trainingPlanExecution(key, f.session().sessionId)!;
+    assert.equal(confirmed.execution.trainingSessionId, "skip-run");
+    // A separate empty course can still commit and replay with the gate closed.
+    f.main.database.prepare("UPDATE training_sessions SET anchor_start_at='2026-01-01T00:00:00Z'").run();
+    const skip = f.body({ action: "skip", reason: "empty" }, "synthetic-interval-session");
+    const result = await handlers.submit(req(skip), reply() as unknown as FastifyReply);
+    const before = f.snapshot(), leaseCount = leases;
+    admitted = false;
+    assert.deepEqual(await handlers.submit(req(skip), reply() as unknown as FastifyReply), result);
+    assert.equal(leases, leaseCount);
+    assert.deepEqual(f.snapshot(), before);
+  } finally { f.close(); }
+});

+function businessSnapshot(db: DatabaseSync) {
+  return Object.fromEntries(["mobile_goals", "mobile_goals_history", "mobile_training_state", "mobile_training_state_history",
+    "mobile_training_session_history", "mobile_training_execution", "mobile_training_execution_history", "mobile_plan_operations"]
+    .map(table => [table, db.prepare(`SELECT * FROM ${table} ORDER BY rowid`).all()]));
+}
+function stopExecution(f: ReturnType<typeof setup>, status: "paused" | "ended") {
+  const row = f.row();
+  f.goals.withPublicationTransaction(() => f.goals.commitTrainingPlanCommandState({ scope: key, expectedRevision: row.revision,
+    state: { ...f.state(), runState: "paused", ...(status === "ended" ? { sourceStatus: "ended" as const } : {}) }, eventType: "fixture:stop" }));
+}
+for (const status of ["paused", "ended"] as const) {
+  for (const action of ["confirm", "correct", "skip"] as const) test(`EXEC-01: ${status} rejects new ${action} with stable 409 and zero business writes`, () => {
+    const f = setup();
+    try {
+      const source = f.facts()[0]!;
+      if (action === "correct") f.execute({ action: "confirm", trainingSessionId: source.trainingSessionId, sourceFactDigest: source.sourceFactDigest });
+      stopExecution(f, status);
+      f.setFacts(action === "skip" ? [] : f.facts());
+      const request = f.body(action === "skip" ? { action, reason: "stopped" }
+        : { action, trainingSessionId: source.trainingSessionId, sourceFactDigest: source.sourceFactDigest });
+      const before = businessSnapshot(f.db);
+      assert.throws(() => f.service.submit(request), (error: unknown) => error instanceof PlanOperationError &&
+        error.statusCode === 409 && error.code === "training_execution_not_active");
+      assert.deepEqual(businessSnapshot(f.db), before);
+    } finally { f.close(); }
+  });
+  test(`EXEC-01: ${status} permits saved-identity Retract and committed Confirm replay without authorizing execution`, () => {
+    const f = setup();
+    try {
+      const source = f.facts()[0]!;
+      const request = f.body({ action: "confirm", trainingSessionId: source.trainingSessionId, sourceFactDigest: source.sourceFactDigest });
+      const confirmed = f.service.submit(request);
+      stopExecution(f, status);
+      const before = businessSnapshot(f.db);
+      assert.deepEqual(f.service.submit(request), confirmed);
+      assert.deepEqual(businessSnapshot(f.db), before);
+      f.setFacts([]);
+      const result = f.execute({ action: "retract", trainingSessionId: source.trainingSessionId, sourceFactDigest: source.sourceFactDigest });
+      assert.equal(result.execution!.association, "invalidated");
+      assert.equal(f.state().runState, "paused");
+      assert.equal(f.goals.trainingPlanExecutionHistory(key, f.session().sessionId).length, 2);
+    } finally { f.close(); }
+  });
+}

+for (const action of ["confirm", "correct"] as const) {
+  for (const point of ["assessment", "receipt", "no-change receipt"] as const) {
+    if (action === "confirm" && point === "no-change receipt") continue;
+    test(`EXEC-02: ${action} detects real fact change at ${point} and rolls back all control rows`, async () => {
+      const f = await skipFixture();
+      try {
+        f.addRun();
+        f.provider.workout = (id, day, asOf) => f.reads.use(store => store.executionWorkout(id, day, asOf));
+        let source = f.provider.candidates("2026-10-26", "2026-10-30")[0]!;
+        if (action === "correct") {
+          f.service.submit(f.body({ action: "confirm", trainingSessionId: source.trainingSessionId, sourceFactDigest: source.sourceFactDigest }));
+          if (point !== "no-change receipt") {
+            f.main.database.exec("UPDATE training_sessions SET anchor_distance_km=6");
+            source = f.provider.candidates("2026-10-26", "2026-10-30")[0]!;
+          }
+        }
+        const mutate = () => f.main.database.exec("UPDATE training_sessions SET anchor_distance_km=7");
+        if (point === "assessment") {
+          const original = f.provider.workout;
+          f.provider.workout = (id, day, asOf) => { const value = original(id, day, asOf); mutate(); return value; };
+        } else {
+          f.control.function("exec02_mutate", () => { mutate(); return 1; });
+          f.control.exec(`CREATE TRIGGER exec02_mutate AFTER INSERT ON mobile_plan_operations BEGIN SELECT exec02_mutate(); END`);
+        }
+        const request = f.body({ action, trainingSessionId: source.trainingSessionId, sourceFactDigest: source.sourceFactDigest });
+        const before = businessSnapshot(f.db);
+        assert.throws(() => f.service.submit(request), code("training_execution_source_changed"));
+        assert.deepEqual(businessSnapshot(f.db), before);
+        assert.equal(f.db.isTransaction, false);
+        assert.equal((f.goals.publicationResult(key, request.operationId) as { status: string }).status, "notFound");
+      } finally { f.close(); }
+    });
+  }
+}

+test("EXEC-02: candidate and assessment use the same source identity and recorded duration", async () => {
+  const f = await skipFixture();

+    f.addRun();

+for (const mismatch of ["id", "digest", "distance", "duration"] as const) test(`EXEC-02: ${mismatch} mismatch in workout assessment rejects without partial claim`, async () => {
+  const f = await skipFixture();
+  try {
+    f.addRun();
+    const source = f.provider.candidates("2026-10-26", "2026-10-30")[0]!;
+    f.provider.workout = (id, day, asOf) => {
+      const value = f.reads.use(store => store.executionWorkout(id, day, asOf))!;
+      return { ...value, ...(mismatch === "id" ? { id: "another-run" } : {}),
+        ...(mismatch === "distance" ? { distanceMeters: 100 } : {}),
+        ...(mismatch === "duration" ? { durationSeconds: 100 } : {}),
+        evidenceRef: { ...value.evidenceRef, revisionOrHash: mismatch === "digest" ? "0".repeat(64) : source.sourceFactDigest } };
+    };
+    const before = businessSnapshot(f.db);
+    assert.throws(() => f.service.submit(f.body({ action: "confirm", trainingSessionId: source.trainingSessionId,
+      sourceFactDigest: source.sourceFactDigest })), code("training_execution_source_changed"));
+    assert.deepEqual(businessSnapshot(f.db), before);
+  } finally { f.close(); }
+});
+
+test("EXEC-03: a fact restored after Reconcile receipt insertion rolls back invalidation and history", async () => {
+  const f = await skipFixture();
+  try {
+    f.addRun();
+    const source = f.provider.candidates("2026-10-26", "2026-10-30")[0]!;
+    f.service.submit(f.body({ action: "confirm", trainingSessionId: source.trainingSessionId, sourceFactDigest: source.sourceFactDigest }));
+    f.main.database.exec("UPDATE training_sessions SET status='inactive'");
+    const before = businessSnapshot(f.db);
+    f.control.function("exec03_recover", () => { f.main.database.exec("UPDATE training_sessions SET status='active'"); return 1; });
+    f.control.exec(`CREATE TRIGGER exec03_recover AFTER INSERT ON mobile_plan_operations BEGIN SELECT exec03_recover(); END`);
+    assert.throws(() => f.service.reconcileSourceChanges(), code("training_execution_source_changed"));
+    assert.deepEqual(businessSnapshot(f.db), before);
+  } finally { f.close(); }
+});

+test("EXEC-03: HTTP Confirm and background Reconcile use one non-nested fact lease through COMMIT", async () => {
+  const f = await skipFixture(), app = Fastify();
+  try {
+    const mutations = new MutationCoordinator(), admission = new TaskAdmissionScheduler(mutations);
+    const worker = admission.owner("fact-writer");
+    let acquisitions = 0;
+    const handlers = createTrainingExecutionCommandHandlers(f.service, () => {}, async fn => {
+      acquisitions++;
+      const release = await mutations.acquireWhenAvailable("execution");
+      try { assert.equal(worker.acquire(), null); return await fn(); }
+      finally { worker.withdraw(); release(); }
+    });
+    app.post("/execution", handlers.submit);
+    let release = worker.acquire()!;
+    const background = handlers.reconcile();
+    await new Promise(resolve => setImmediate(resolve));
+    f.addRun(); release();
+    assert.deepEqual(await background, { changed: 0 });
+    const fact = f.provider.candidates("2026-10-26", "2026-10-30")[0]!;
+    const request = f.body({ action: "confirm", trainingSessionId: fact.trainingSessionId, sourceFactDigest: fact.sourceFactDigest });
+    const response = await app.inject({ method: "POST", url: "/execution", payload: request });
+    assert.equal(response.statusCode, 200);
+    release = worker.acquire()!;
+    const reconcile = handlers.reconcile();
+    await new Promise(resolve => setImmediate(resolve));
+    f.main.database.exec("UPDATE training_sessions SET anchor_distance_km=6");
+    release();
+    assert.deepEqual(await reconcile, { changed: 1 });
+    assert.equal(acquisitions, 3, "one acquisition per entry; no nested service lease");
+    const before = businessSnapshot(f.db);
+    const replay = await app.inject({ method: "POST", url: "/execution", payload: request });
+    assert.equal(replay.statusCode, 200);
+    assert.deepEqual(replay.json(), response.json());
+    assert.equal(acquisitions, 3, "committed replay needs no lease or facts");
+    assert.deepEqual(businessSnapshot(f.db), before);
+  } finally { await app.close(); f.close(); }
+});
+
+test("EXEC-02/03: an independent CLI process respects the API publication lock, then Reconcile sees its committed fact", async () => {
+  const f = await skipFixture(), app = Fastify();
+  let release: (() => void) | undefined;
+  try {
+    f.addRun();
+    const mainPath = join(f.root, "facts.sqlite");
+    const child = () => spawnSync(process.execPath, ["--import", "tsx", "--input-type=module", "-e", `
+      import { acquireDatabasePublicationLock } from '@rai/storage-sqlite';
+      import { DatabaseSync } from 'node:sqlite';
+      let release;
+      try {
+        release = acquireDatabasePublicationLock(process.argv[1], 'cli');
+        const db = new DatabaseSync(process.argv[1]);
+        db.exec('BEGIN IMMEDIATE');
+        db.exec('UPDATE training_sessions SET anchor_distance_km=6');
+        db.exec('COMMIT'); db.close();
+      } catch { process.exitCode = 9; }
+      finally { release?.(); }
+    `, mainPath], { encoding: "utf8" });
+    release = acquireDatabasePublicationLock(mainPath, "api");
+    const mutations = new MutationCoordinator();
+    const handlers = createTrainingExecutionCommandHandlers(f.service, () => {}, async fn => {
+      const unlock = await mutations.acquireWhenAvailable("execution");
+      try { return await fn(); } finally { unlock(); }
+    });
+    app.post("/execution", handlers.submit);
+    f.provider.workout = (id, day, asOf) => {
+      assert.equal(child().status, 9, "supported CLI writer cannot enter while API owns main DB");
+      return f.reads.use(store => store.executionWorkout(id, day, asOf));
+    };
+    const source = f.provider.candidates("2026-10-26", "2026-10-30")[0]!;
+    const response = await app.inject({ method: "POST", url: "/execution", payload: f.body({ action: "confirm",
+      trainingSessionId: source.trainingSessionId, sourceFactDigest: source.sourceFactDigest }) });
+    assert.equal(response.statusCode, 200);
+    release(); release = undefined;
+    assert.equal(child().status, 0, "CLI commits only after API releases the publication lock");
+    release = acquireDatabasePublicationLock(mainPath, "api");
+    assert.equal((await handlers.reconcile()).changed, 1);
+    const history = f.goals.trainingPlanExecutionHistory(key, f.session().sessionId);
+    assert.equal(history.length, 2);
+    assert.equal(history[0]!.execution.sourceFactDigest, source.sourceFactDigest);
+    assert.equal(history[1]!.execution.association, "invalidated");
+  } finally { release?.(); await app.close(); f.close(); }
+});
+
+test("EXEC-03: recovery between pre-scan and transaction cannot persist a no-change receipt that masks later deletion", async () => {
+  const f = await skipFixture();
+  try {
+    f.addRun();
+    const source = f.provider.candidates("2026-10-26", "2026-10-30")[0]!;
+    f.service.submit(f.body({ action: "confirm", trainingSessionId: source.trainingSessionId, sourceFactDigest: source.sourceFactDigest }));
+    const original = f.provider.candidates;
+    f.main.database.exec("UPDATE training_sessions SET status='inactive'");
+    let calls = 0;
+    f.provider.candidates = (start, end) => {
+      const values = original(start, end);
+      if (++calls === 1) f.main.database.exec("UPDATE training_sessions SET status='active'");
+      return values;
+    };
+    const before = businessSnapshot(f.db);
+    let raceError: unknown;
+    try { f.service.reconcileSourceChanges(); } catch (error) { raceError = error; }
+    const afterRace = businessSnapshot(f.db);
+    f.provider.candidates = original;
+    f.main.database.exec("UPDATE training_sessions SET status='inactive'");
+    assert.equal(f.service.reconcileSourceChanges().changed, 1, "later deletion must not replay a poisoned no-change receipt");
+    assert.ok(code("training_execution_source_changed")(raceError));
+    assert.deepEqual(afterRace, before, "no no-change operation receipt may survive this fact race");
+    assert.equal(f.goals.trainingPlanExecutionHistory(key, f.session().sessionId).length, 2);
+  } finally { f.close(); }
+});

+test("EXEC-03: legacy reconcile no-change receipt remains immutable without blocking a new v2 evaluation", async () => {
+  const f = await skipFixture();
+  try {
+    f.addRun();
+    const source = f.provider.candidates("2026-10-26", "2026-10-30")[0]!;

+        trainingSessionId: execution.trainingSessionId, sourceFactDigest: execution.sourceFactDigest }] }).slice(0, 40)}`;
+    const input = { operationId, operationScope: scope };
+    f.goals.publicationOperation(key, operationId, "training-plan-execution-command", input, () => ({ changed: 0 }));
+    const saved = f.db.prepare("SELECT * FROM mobile_plan_operations WHERE operation_id=?").get(operationId);

+  } finally { f.close(); }

+import assert from "node:assert/strict";
+import test from "node:test";
+import type { FastifyReply, FastifyRequest } from "fastify";
+import {
+  createTrainingExecutionCommandHandlers,
+  type TrainingPlanExecutionCommandService
+} from "../src/training-plan-execution-commands.js";
+import { ExecutionCursorError } from "../src/training-plan-execution-match.js";
+
+test("B06/S03-02: an invalid candidate cursor is a 422 command error, never a storage 503", async () => {
+  const service = { candidates: () => {
+    throw new ExecutionCursorError("执行候选游标与当前查询不匹配，请重新读取候选。");
+  } } as unknown as TrainingPlanExecutionCommandService;
+  const handlers = createTrainingExecutionCommandHandlers(service, () => {}, async (fn) => fn());
+  const request = { params: { sessionId: "session-1" }, query: {}, headers: {}, id: "cursor-test",
+    log: { error: () => {} } } as unknown as FastifyRequest;
+  const replyState = {
+    statusCode: 200,
+    body: null as unknown,
+    header() { return this; },
+    code(value: number) { this.statusCode = value; return this; },
+    send(value: unknown) { this.body = value; return this; }
+  };
+  await handlers.candidates(request, replyState as unknown as FastifyReply);
+  assert.equal(replyState.statusCode, 422);
+  assert.deepEqual(replyState.body, {
+    code: "training_session_command_invalid",
+    message: "执行候选游标与当前查询不匹配，请重新读取候选。"
+  });
+});

+  const f = seed();
+  try {
+    f.db.prepare("UPDATE mobile_training_state_history SET payload_json=? WHERE scope=?").run(JSON.stringify({ ...f.state, lockedSession: lock }), scope);
+    const before = snapshot(f.db);
+    const args = ["--import", "tsx", "scripts/migrate-training-planner.mts", "--control-db", f.path,
+      "--server-id", identity.serverId, "--athlete-id", identity.athleteId, "--dataset-epoch", identity.datasetEpoch, "--session-identities"];
+    for (const extra of [[], ["--apply", "--expect-source-digest", "0".repeat(64)]]) {
+      const result = spawnSync(process.execPath, [...args, ...extra], { encoding: "utf8" });
+      assert.equal(result.status, 1);
+      const report = JSON.parse(result.stdout);
+      assert.equal(report.error.code, "session_migration_locked_rest_conflict");
+      assert.ok(report.conflicts.some((c: { revision: number; localDate: string }) => c.revision === 1 && c.localDate === date));
+      assert.equal(result.stdout.includes("synthetic lock"), false);
+      assert.deepEqual(snapshot(f.db), before);
+    }
+  } finally { f.close(); }

+test("A-01-01: legal rest lock migrates deterministically without duplicate explicit_rest and keeps history", () => {
+  const f = seed();

+    f.goals.bindTrainingPlanOwner(() => identity);
+    f.goals.lockSession({ ...lock, session: { ...lock.session, kind: "rest" } });
+    const original = f.store.read(scope)!.state;
+    const before = snapshot(f.db);
+    assert.equal(auditTrainingPlanStateCopy(f.path).ok, true);
+    const ready = inspectSessionIdentityMigration(f.db, scope);
+    assert.equal(ready.status, "ready");
+    assert.deepEqual(snapshot(f.db), before, "inspect is read only");
+    assert.throws(() => applySessionIdentityMigration(f.db, scope, "0".repeat(64), ready.existingRevision),
+      (error: unknown) => (error as { code: string }).code === "source_digest_mismatch");
+    assert.deepEqual(snapshot(f.db), before, "failed migration writes no tables or DDL");
+    applySessionIdentityMigration(f.db, scope, ready.sourceDigest, ready.existingRevision);
+    const after = f.store.read(scope)!.state;
+    assert.equal(after.schemaVersion, 2);
+    assert.deepEqual(after.lockedSession, original.lockedSession);
+    if (after.schemaVersion !== 2) throw new Error("schema 2 required");
+    assert.equal(after.suppressions.some(s => s.localDate === date), false);
+    for (const table of ["mobile_training_state_history", "mobile_goals_history"]) {
+      const oldRows = before.find(row => row.name === table)!.rows!;
+      assert.deepEqual(snapshot(f.db).find(row => row.name === table)!.rows!.slice(0, oldRows.length), oldRows);
+    }
+    assert.equal(auditTrainingPlanStateCopy(f.path).ok, true);
+    const replay = inspectSessionIdentityMigration(f.db, scope);
+    assert.equal(replay.status, "replayed");
+    const committed = snapshot(f.db);
+    applySessionIdentityMigration(f.db, scope, replay.sourceDigest, replay.existingRevision);
+    assert.deepEqual(snapshot(f.db), committed);
+  } finally { f.close(); }
+});

+      "set_rest stays idempotent and does not rewrite its decision");

+test("P2.4: move/cancel/set_rest replace only their own suppression and never another course's tombstone", () => {
+  const f = setup();
+  try {
+    // The initial course moves away from 10-28, leaving a moved tombstone there.
+    f.service.submit(f.command({ action: "move", ...f.existing(), localDate: "2026-10-29" }));
+    const initialTombstone = structuredClone(f.state().suppressions.find(item =>
+      item.localDate === "2026-10-28" && item.sessionId === fixture.initial.sessionId)!);
+    // A new course is added elsewhere, then moved onto the tombstoned source date.
+    // Move must not release the tombstone; only add may explicitly release it.
+    const added = f.service.submit(f.command({ action: "add", prescription: easy("2026-10-30", 8) as never })).session!;
+    f.service.submit(f.command({ action: "move", sessionId: added.sessionId, sessionRevision: added.sessionRevision, localDate: "2026-10-28" }));
+    assert.deepEqual(f.state().suppressions.find(item => item.localDate === "2026-10-28" && item.sessionId === fixture.initial.sessionId),
+      initialTombstone, "moving onto the date never deletes another course's tombstone");
+    // Moving the new course exits 10-28; its own tombstone is added while the other remains.
+    const atSource = f.state().sessions.find(item => item.sessionId === added.sessionId)!;
+    f.service.submit(f.command({ action: "move", sessionId: added.sessionId, sessionRevision: atSource.sessionRevision, localDate: "2026-10-31" }));
+    const moved = f.state();
+    assert.equal(moved.suppressions.filter(item => item.localDate === "2026-10-28" && item.reason === "moved").length, 2);
+    assert.deepEqual(moved.suppressions.find(item => item.localDate === "2026-10-28" && item.sessionId === fixture.initial.sessionId),
+      initialTombstone);
+    // Move it back and cancel it on 10-28: its cancelled tombstone must not erase the first one.
+    const back = f.state().sessions.find(item => item.sessionId === added.sessionId)!;
+    f.service.submit(f.command({ action: "move", sessionId: added.sessionId, sessionRevision: back.sessionRevision, localDate: "2026-10-28" }));
+    const current = f.state().sessions.find(item => item.sessionId === added.sessionId)!;
+    f.service.submit(f.command({ action: "cancel", sessionId: added.sessionId, sessionRevision: current.sessionRevision }));
+    assert.deepEqual(f.state().suppressions.find(item => item.localDate === "2026-10-28" && item.sessionId === fixture.initial.sessionId),
+      initialTombstone, "cancel only replaces the cancelled course's own suppression");
+    // set_rest adds explicit rest without deleting either tombstone.
+    f.service.submit(f.command({ action: "set_rest", localDate: "2026-10-28" }));
+    const final = f.state().suppressions.filter(item => item.localDate === "2026-10-28");
+    assert.equal(final.length, 3);
+    assert.ok(final.some(item => item.reason === "explicit_rest" && item.sessionId === null));
+    assert.ok(final.some(item => item.reason === "moved" && item.sessionId === fixture.initial.sessionId));
+    assert.ok(final.some(item => item.reason === "cancelled" && item.sessionId === added.sessionId));
+  } finally { f.close(); }
+});

+test("D-01: historical rounding cannot mask changed steps, type, targets or fabricated totals", () => {
+  for (const item of distanceFixture.cases.filter(item => item.generated || item.id === "historical-distance-only")) {
+    const f = setup(item.prescription, distanceZones);

+      targeted.blocks[0]!.steps[0]!.alert = { kind: "heart_rate", lower: 100, upper: 150 };
+      for (const patch of [
+        { structure }, { structure: targeted },

+        { distanceKm: item.prescription.distanceKm! + 0.1 }, { distanceKm: 0 }
+      ]) assert.throws(() => f.service.preview(f.request({ action: "edit", ...f.existing(), patch: { ...patch,
+        distanceKm: "distanceKm" in patch ? patch.distanceKm : item.prescription.distanceKm } as never })), code("training_session_command_invalid"), item.id);
+      assert.deepEqual(f.snapshot(), before);
+    } finally { f.close(); }
+  }
+});

+test("D-02: omitted distance delegates changed mixed dose to server; null/zero/rounded explicit totals stay strict", () => {
+  const item = distanceFixture.cases.find(item => item.id === "mixed")!;
+  const f = setup(item.prescription, distanceZones);
+  try {
+    const structure = structuredClone(item.prescription.structure!);
+    structure.blocks[0]!.iterations = 5;
+    const measured = measureStructure(structure, "interval", pacesFromZones(distanceZones));
+    const draft: TrainingPlanSessionDraft = { action: "edit", ...f.existing(), patch: { structure, reason: "one extra repetition" } };
+    const request = f.request(draft), before = f.snapshot();
+    const preview = f.service.preview(request);
+    const expected = Math.round(measured.distanceKm * 1000) / 1000;
+    assert.equal(preview.impact.after!.prescription.distanceKm, expected);
+    assert.deepEqual(preview.impact.after!.prescription.structure, structure);
+    assert.deepEqual(f.snapshot(), before);
+    for (const distanceKm of [null, 0, 5, roundKm(measured.distanceKm)]) {
+      assert.throws(() => f.service.preview(f.request({ ...draft, patch: { structure, distanceKm } })), code("training_session_command_invalid"));
+    }
+    const command = { ...request, candidateId: preview.candidateId, operationId: randomUUID() };
+    f.facts();
+    assert.throws(() => f.service.submit(command), code("training_session_candidate_mismatch"));
+    assert.deepEqual(f.snapshot(), before, "failed submit writes no partial state, history or operation");
+    const result = f.service.submit(f.command(draft));
+    assert.equal(result.session!.prescription.distanceKm, expected);
+    assert.deepEqual(result.session!.prescription.structure, structure);
+  } finally { f.close(); }
+});

+test("D-02: add omission computes on server; pure time keeps null; explicit old-client exact totals remain accepted", () => {
+  const f = setup();
+  const service = new TrainingPlanSessionCommandService(f.goals, () => scope, () => "2026-10-25", () => ({
+    fingerprint: "distance-fixture", prescription: distanceZones, validate: () => {} }));
+  try {
+    for (const item of distanceFixture.cases) {
+      const { distanceKm, ...prescription } = item.prescription;
+      const request = f.request({ action: "add", prescription: { ...prescription, localDate: "2026-11-02" } as never });
+      const preview = service.preview(request);
+      const hasDistance = JSON.stringify(prescription.structure).includes('"type":"distance"');
+      assert.equal(preview.impact.after!.prescription.distanceKm,
+        hasDistance ? Math.round(item.measured.distanceKm * 1000) / 1000 : null, item.id);
+      const exact = { ...prescription, localDate: "2026-11-02", distanceKm: hasDistance ? Math.round(item.measured.distanceKm * 1000) / 1000 : null };
+      assert.equal(service.preview(f.request({ action: "add", prescription: exact as never })).impact.after!.prescription.distanceKm, exact.distanceKm);
+    }
+    const before = f.snapshot();
+    for (const prescription of [distanceStructure("2026-11-02", 80001, 80.001),
+      { ...timeOnly("2026-11-02"), structure: { warmup: null, cooldown: null, blocks: [{ iterations: 2, steps: [{ purpose: "work", goal: { type: "time", seconds: 14400 }, alert: null }] }] } }]) {
+      assert.throws(() => service.preview(f.request({ action: "add", prescription: prescription as never })), code("training_session_command_invalid"));
+    }
+    assert.deepEqual(f.snapshot(), before);
+  } finally { f.close(); }
+});

+test("D-01/D-02: shared native omission/null/value payloads preserve unchanged dose; legal structure adjustments remeasure", () => {
+  for (const item of distanceFixture.cases) {
+    const f = setup(item.prescription, distanceZones);
+    try {
+      const { localDate, distanceKm, ...content } = item.prescription;
+      const patch = { ...content, ...(item.editorDistanceMode === "omit" ? {} : { distanceKm }) };
+      const preview = f.service.preview(f.request({ action: "edit", ...f.existing(), patch: patch as never }));
+      assert.deepEqual(preview.impact.after!.prescription, item.prescription, item.id);
+      const structure = structuredClone(item.prescription.structure!);
+      structure.blocks[0]!.iterations += 1;
+      const measured = measureStructure(structure, item.prescription.kind, pacesFromZones(distanceZones));
+      const adjusted = f.service.submit(f.command({ action: "edit", ...f.existing(), patch: { structure } }));
+      const hasDistance = JSON.stringify(structure).includes('"type":"distance"');
+      assert.equal(adjusted.session!.prescription.distanceKm, hasDistance ? Math.round(measured.distanceKm * 1000) / 1000 : null, item.id);
+      assert.deepEqual(adjusted.session!.prescription.structure, structure, "measurement never scales the requested structure");
+    } finally { f.close(); }
+  }
+});

+test("D-01: unchanged but corrupt historical totals are not accepted as legacy rounding", () => {

+test("D-02: COMMIT failure rolls back a server-measured mixed edit, projection, override and operation", context => {
+  const item = distanceFixture.cases.find(item => item.id === "mixed")!;
+  const f = setup(item.prescription, distanceZones);
+  try {
+    const structure = structuredClone(item.prescription.structure!);
+    structure.blocks[0]!.iterations = 5;
+    const body = f.command({ action: "edit", ...f.existing(), patch: { structure } }), before = f.snapshot();
+    const original = DatabaseSync.prototype.exec;
+    let fail = true;
+    const mock = context.mock.method(DatabaseSync.prototype, "exec", function(this: DatabaseSync, sql: string) {
+      if (sql === "COMMIT" && f.goals.publicationInFlight() && fail) { fail = false; throw new Error("injected measured-dose COMMIT failure"); }
+      return original.call(this, sql);
+    });
+    assert.throws(() => f.service.submit(body), /injected measured-dose COMMIT failure/);
+    mock.mock.restore();
+    assert.deepEqual(f.snapshot(), before, "all six business/history/result tables remain unchanged");
+    assert.equal(f.goals.publicationGuardState().pendingSince, null);
+    const result = f.service.submit(body);
+    assert.equal(result.session!.prescription.distanceKm,
+      Math.round(measureStructure(structure, item.prescription.kind, pacesFromZones(distanceZones)).distanceKm * 1000) / 1000);
+    assert.deepEqual(result.session!.prescription.structure, structure);
+  } finally { f.close(); }
+});

+// D-03: synthetic structures keep quantity tests independent of weekly safety.
+function quantityCourse(steps = 1, blocks = 1, iterations = 1): MobilePlannedDay & { kind: "easy"; structure: NonNullable<MobilePlannedDay["structure"]> } {
+  return { ...easy("2026-10-28"), kind: "easy", distanceKm: null, structure: { warmup: null, cooldown: null,
+    blocks: Array.from({ length: blocks }, () => ({ iterations,
+      steps: Array.from({ length: steps }, () => ({ purpose: "work" as const,
+        goal: { type: "distance" as const, meters: 100 }, alert: null })) })) } };
+}
+const allControlTables = (db: DatabaseSync) => db.prepare(
+  "SELECT name FROM sqlite_master WHERE type='table' AND name NOT LIKE 'sqlite_%' ORDER BY name"
+).all().map(row => ({ table: row.name, rows: db.prepare(`SELECT * FROM "${row.name}" ORDER BY rowid`).all() }));
+
+for (const [label, steps, blocks, iterations, accepted] of [
+  ["8 steps", 8, 1, 1, true], ["9 steps", 9, 1, 1, false],
+  ["8 blocks", 1, 8, 1, true], ["9 blocks", 1, 9, 1, false],
+  ["20 iterations", 1, 1, 20, true], ["21 iterations", 1, 1, 21, false]
+] as const) {
+  for (const action of ["add", "edit"] as const) test(`D-03: direct ${action} ${label} ${accepted ? "accepted" : "rejected"}`, () => {
+    const f = setup();
+    try {
+      const { localDate, distanceKm, ...patch } = quantityCourse(steps, blocks, iterations);
+      const draft: TrainingPlanSessionDraft = action === "add"
+        ? { action, prescription: { localDate: "2026-10-29", ...patch } as never }
+        : { action, ...f.existing(), patch };
+      const request = f.request(draft), before = allControlTables(f.db);
+      if (accepted) {
+        const preview = f.service.preview(request);
+        assert.deepEqual(allControlTables(f.db), before, "preview must write nothing");
+        const command = { ...request, operationId: randomUUID(), candidateId: preview.candidateId };
+        const result = f.service.submit(command);
+        assert.deepEqual(result.session!.prescription.structure, patch.structure);
+        assert.deepEqual(f.service.submit(command), result);
+      } else {
+        assert.throws(() => f.service.preview(request), code("training_session_command_invalid"));
+        assert.throws(() => f.service.submit({ ...request, operationId: randomUUID(), candidateId: "unissued" }),
+          code("training_session_command_invalid"));
+        assert.deepEqual(allControlTables(f.db), before, "every control table, history and receipt stays unchanged");
+      }
+    } finally { f.close(); }
+  });
+}
+
+test("D-03: API preview/submit reject nine steps without writes", async () => {
+  const f = setup(), app = Fastify({ ajv: { customOptions: { removeAdditional: false } } });
+  try {
+    const handlers = createTrainingSessionCommandHandlers(f.service, () => {}, async fn => fn());
+    app.post("/preview", trainingSessionCommandRouteOptions(false), handlers.preview);
+    app.post("/submit", trainingSessionCommandRouteOptions(true), handlers.submit);
+    const { distanceKm, ...prescription } = quantityCourse(9);
+    const request = f.request({ action: "add", prescription: { ...prescription, localDate: "2026-10-29" } as never });
+    const before = allControlTables(f.db);
+    const preview = await app.inject({ method: "POST", url: "/preview", payload: request });
+    assert.equal(preview.statusCode, 422, preview.body);
+    assert.equal(preview.json().code, "training_session_command_invalid");
+    const submit = await app.inject({ method: "POST", url: "/submit", payload: {
+      ...request, operationId: randomUUID(), candidateId: "unissued" } });
+    assert.equal(submit.statusCode, 422, submit.body);
+    assert.equal(submit.json().code, "training_session_command_invalid");
+    assert.deepEqual(allControlTables(f.db), before);
+  } finally { await app.close(); f.close(); }
+});

+test("D-03: persisted nine-step courses remain lossless; metadata edits survive, dose edits require legal replacement", () => {
+  const original = quantityCourse(9); original.distanceKm = 0.9;
+  const f = setup(original);
+  try {
+    assert.deepEqual(f.state().sessions[0]!.prescription, original);
+    assert.deepEqual(f.goals.read().weekPlan[0], original);
+    const oldHistory = f.db.prepare("SELECT * FROM mobile_training_session_history ORDER BY rowid").all();
+    f.service.submit(f.command({ action: "edit", ...f.existing(), patch: { reason: "preserve all nine historical steps" } }));
+    assert.deepEqual(f.state().sessions[0]!.prescription.structure, original.structure);
+    f.service.submit(f.command({ action: "move", ...f.existing(), localDate: "2026-10-29" }));
+    assert.deepEqual(f.state().sessions[0]!.prescription.structure, original.structure);
+    const before = allControlTables(f.db);
+    for (const patch of [{ kind: "interval" as const }, { distanceKm: 0.9 }, { structure: original.structure }]) {
+      assert.throws(() => f.service.preview(f.request({ action: "edit", ...f.existing(), patch })), code("training_session_command_invalid"));
+    }
+    assert.deepEqual(allControlTables(f.db), before);
+    const replacement = quantityCourse(8).structure!;
+    f.service.submit(f.command({ action: "edit", ...f.existing(), patch: { structure: replacement } }));
+    assert.deepEqual(f.state().sessions[0]!.prescription.structure, replacement);
+    assert.deepEqual(f.db.prepare("SELECT * FROM mobile_training_session_history ORDER BY rowid").all().slice(0, oldHistory.length), oldHistory,
+      "repair appends a new revision; old prescriptions are not rewritten");
+  } finally { f.close(); }
+});
+
+
+for (const [label, goals, iterations, accepted] of [
+  ["80 km", [80_000], 1, true], ["80 km plus one metre", [80_001], 1, false],
+  ["80 km through iterations", [4_000], 20, true], ["over 80 km through iterations", [4_001], 20, false],
+  ["4 hours", [14_400], 1, true], ["step over 4 hours", [14_401], 1, false],
+  ["4 hours through steps", [7_200, 7_200], 1, true], ["over 4 hours through steps", [7_200, 7_201], 1, false],
+  ["4 hours through iterations", [720], 20, true], ["over 4 hours through iterations", [721], 20, false]
+] as const) test(`D-03: dose boundary ${label}`, () => {
+  const isTime = label.includes("hours");
+  const f = setup(undefined, { ...distanceZones!, easyPaceUpper: isTime ? 400 : 150 });
+  try {
+    const prescription = { localDate: "2026-10-29", kind: "easy" as const, structure: { warmup: null, cooldown: null,
+      blocks: [{ iterations, steps: goals.map(amount => ({ purpose: "work" as const, alert: null,
+        goal: isTime ? { type: "time" as const, seconds: amount } : { type: "distance" as const, meters: amount } })) }] } };
+    const request = f.request({ action: "add", prescription }), before = allControlTables(f.db);
+    if (accepted) {
+      const preview = f.service.preview(request);
+      assert.deepEqual(allControlTables(f.db), before);
+      const result = f.service.submit({ ...request, operationId: randomUUID(), candidateId: preview.candidateId });
+      assert.deepEqual(result.session!.prescription.structure, prescription.structure);
+    } else {
+      assert.throws(() => f.service.preview(request), code("training_session_command_invalid"));
+      assert.deepEqual(allControlTables(f.db), before);
+    }
+  } finally { f.close(); }
+});

+test("D-03: lossless shape reader retains single-step metre/second ceilings", () => {

+test("D-03: a committed historical nine-step operation replays ahead of schema/capability/new-write gates", async () => {
+  const f = setup(), app = Fastify({ ajv: { customOptions: { removeAdditional: false } } });
+  try {
+    const { distanceKm, ...prescription } = quantityCourse(9);
+    const request = { ...f.request({ action: "add", prescription: { ...prescription, localDate: "2026-10-29" } }),
+      operationId: randomUUID(), candidateId: "historical-candidate" };
+    // Seed an old immutable receipt, not a new business write under today's validator.
+    const historicalSession = { ...structuredClone(fixture.initial), prescription: { ...prescription, distanceKm: 0.9 } };
+    const result = { contractVersion: 1, operationId: request.operationId, stateRevision: 2, goalsRevision: 1,
+      planInstanceId: request.planInstanceId, outcome: "changed", session: historicalSession, execution: null,
+      planUpdate: "pending", warnings: [] };
+    f.db.prepare("INSERT INTO mobile_plan_operations(scope,operation_id,request_digest,result_json,expires_at) VALUES(?,?,?,?,?)")
+      .run(key, request.operationId, digest({ route: "training-plan-session-command", input: request }), JSON.stringify(result), "2099-01-01T00:00:00.000Z");
+    const before = allControlTables(f.db);
+    f.busy();
+    const handlers = createTrainingSessionCommandHandlers(f.service, () => { throw new Error("replay must not admit"); },
+      async () => { throw new Error("replay must not capture facts"); });
+    app.post("/submit", trainingSessionCommandRouteOptions(true), handlers.submit);
+    assert.deepEqual(f.service.submit(request), result);
+    const replay = await app.inject({ method: "POST", url: "/submit", payload: request });
+    assert.equal(replay.statusCode, 200, replay.body);
+    assert.deepEqual(replay.json(), result);
+    assert.deepEqual(allControlTables(f.db), before);
+    const fresh = await app.inject({ method: "POST", url: "/submit", payload: { ...request, operationId: randomUUID() } });
+    assert.equal(fresh.statusCode, 422);
+    assert.equal(fresh.json().code, "training_session_command_invalid");
+    assert.deepEqual(allControlTables(f.db), before);
+  } finally { await app.close(); f.close(); }
+});

+test("D-03: production route options share OpenAPI schemas and reject coercion without changing errors", async () => {
+  const f = setup(), app = Fastify({ ajv: { customOptions: { removeAdditional: false } } });
+  try {
+    const handlers = createTrainingSessionCommandHandlers(f.service, () => {}, async fn => fn());
+    app.post("/preview", trainingSessionCommandRouteOptions(false), handlers.preview);
+    app.post("/submit", trainingSessionCommandRouteOptions(true), handlers.submit);
+    const { distanceKm, ...prescription } = quantityCourse(8);
+    const request = f.request({ action: "add", prescription: { ...prescription, localDate: "2026-10-29" } });
+    const before = allControlTables(f.db);
+    for (const input of [
+      { ...request, expectedStateRevision: String(request.expectedStateRevision) },
+      { ...request, extra: true }, { ...request, contractVersion: 2 },
+      { ...request, draft: { ...request.draft, prescription: { ...prescription, structure: {
+        ...prescription.structure, blocks: [{ ...prescription.structure.blocks[0], iterations: "1" }] } } } }
+    ]) {
+      const response = await app.inject({ method: "POST", url: "/preview", payload: input });
+      assert.equal(response.statusCode, 422, response.body);
+      assert.equal(response.headers["cache-control"], "no-store");
+      assert.equal(response.json().code, input.contractVersion === 2 ? "training_session_contract_required" : "training_session_command_invalid");
+      assert.deepEqual(allControlTables(f.db), before);
+    }
+    const preview = await app.inject({ method: "POST", url: "/preview", payload: request });
+    assert.equal(preview.statusCode, 200, preview.body);
+    const command = { ...request, operationId: randomUUID(), candidateId: preview.json().candidateId };
+    const invalid = structuredClone(command);
+    if (invalid.draft.action !== "add") throw new Error("add expected");
+    invalid.draft.prescription.structure.blocks[0]!.steps.push({ purpose: "work", goal: { type: "distance", meters: 100 }, alert: null });
+    const rejected = await app.inject({ method: "POST", url: "/submit", payload: invalid });
+    assert.equal(rejected.statusCode, 422, rejected.body);
+    assert.deepEqual(allControlTables(f.db), before);
+    const submitted = await app.inject({ method: "POST", url: "/submit", payload: command });
+    assert.equal(submitted.statusCode, 200, submitted.body);
+  } finally { await app.close(); f.close(); }
+});

+test("D-03: identity migration and historical replay retain all nine legacy steps", () => {
+  const f = seed();
+  try {
+    const day = { localDate: "2026-10-28", kind: "easy" as const, distanceKm: 0.9, reason: "legacy nine-step course",
+      structure: { warmup: null, cooldown: null, blocks: [{ iterations: 1, steps: Array.from({ length: 9 }, () => ({
+        purpose: "work" as const, goal: { type: "distance" as const, meters: 100 }, alert: null })) }] } };
+    const goals = f.goals.write({ weekPlan: [day] }, { preserveWeekPlan: true });
+    const row = f.store.read(scope)!;
+    f.store.commitState({ scope, expectedRevision: row.revision, state: {
+      ...f.state, weekPlan: [day], lockedSession: null, goalsRevision: goals.revision }, eventType: "fixture:legacy-nine-steps" });
+    const before = snapshot(f.db);
+    const report = inspectSessionIdentityMigration(f.db, scope);
+    assert.deepEqual(snapshot(f.db), before, "dry-run preserves historical JSON and allocates nothing");
+    const history = f.db.prepare("SELECT * FROM mobile_training_state_history ORDER BY revision").all();
+    const migrated = applySessionIdentityMigration(f.db, scope, report.sourceDigest, report.existingRevision);
+    const current = f.store.read(scope)!;
+    assert.equal(current.state.schemaVersion, 2);
+    if (current.state.schemaVersion !== 2) throw new Error("schema 2 required");
+    assert.deepEqual(current.state.sessions[0]!.prescription, day);
+    const repo = new TrainingPlanSessionRepository(f.db);
+    assert.deepEqual(repo.get(scope, migrated.planInstanceId!, current.state.sessions[0]!.sessionId, 1)!.session.prescription, day);
+    assert.deepEqual(f.db.prepare("SELECT * FROM mobile_training_state_history ORDER BY revision").all().slice(0, history.length), history);
+    const after = snapshot(f.db);
+    assert.equal(applySessionIdentityMigration(f.db, scope, report.sourceDigest, report.existingRevision).status, "replayed");
+    assert.deepEqual(snapshot(f.db), after);
+  } finally { f.close(); }
+});
