+import assert from "node:assert/strict";
+import { spawnSync } from "node:child_process";
+import { mkdtempSync, readFileSync, rmSync, existsSync } from "node:fs";
+import { tmpdir } from "node:os";
+import { join } from "node:path";
+import { DatabaseSync } from "node:sqlite";
+import test from "node:test";
+import { auditTrainingPlanStateCopy } from "../src/training-plan-state-audit.js";
+import { parseTrainingPlanState } from "../src/training-plan-store.js";
+
+const fixture = JSON.parse(readFileSync(new URL("../../ios/tests/fixtures/training-plan-sessions-v2.json", import.meta.url), "utf8"));
+const scope = JSON.stringify(["synthetic-server", "synthetic-athlete", "synthetic-epoch"]);
+const date = "2026-10-30";
+const lock = { localDate: date, session: { kind: "easy", distanceKm: 5, reason: "PRIVATE_PAYLOAD_SENTINEL" },
+  verdictTone: "green", lockedAt: "2026-10-25T03:00:00.000Z" };
+const legacy = () => {
+  const { planInstanceId, sessions, suppressions, publishedInputKey, ...state } = fixture.payload2;
+  return parseTrainingPlanState({ ...state, schemaVersion: 1, migrationVersion: 1 });
+};
+function copyFixture() {
+  const root = mkdtempSync(join(tmpdir(), "rai-a01-audit-")), path = join(root, "offline-copy.sqlite");
+  const db = new DatabaseSync(path);
+  // Minimal read schema deliberately permits malformed historical JSON.
+  db.exec("CREATE TABLE mobile_training_state(scope TEXT,revision INTEGER,payload_json TEXT); CREATE TABLE mobile_training_state_history(scope TEXT,revision INTEGER,payload_json TEXT)");
+  const insert = (table: string, payload: unknown, revision = 1, key = scope) =>
+    db.prepare(`INSERT INTO ${table}(scope,revision,payload_json) VALUES (?,?,?)`).run(key, revision, JSON.stringify(payload));
+  return { root, path, db, insert, close() { db.close(); rmSync(root, { recursive: true, force: true }); } };
+}
+
+test("A-01 audit: legal schema 1/2 history and locks report zero conflicts without file writes", () => {
+  const f = copyFixture();
+  try {
+    f.insert("mobile_training_state", { ...fixture.payload2, lockedSession: { ...lock, localDate: "2026-10-29" } }, 2);
+    f.insert("mobile_training_state_history", legacy());
+    const before = readFileSync(f.path), report = auditTrainingPlanStateCopy(f.path);
+    assert.equal(report.ok, true); assert.equal(report.snapshots, 2); assert.equal(report.scopes, 1);
+    assert.equal(report.affectedScopes, 0); assert.deepEqual(report.conflicts, []); assert.deepEqual(report.issues, []);
+    assert.deepEqual(readFileSync(f.path), before);
+  } finally { f.close(); }
+});
+
+test("A-01 audit: both schemas, both conflict kinds and all historical scopes are reported with safe metadata", () => {
+  const f = copyFixture();
+  try {
+    f.insert("mobile_training_state", { ...fixture.payload2, lockedSession: lock }, 2);
+    f.insert("mobile_training_state_history", { ...legacy(), lockedSession: lock }, 1);
+    f.insert("mobile_training_state_history", { ...legacy(), lockedSession: lock }, 4, JSON.stringify(["s2", "a2", "e2"]));
+    const before = readFileSync(f.path), report = auditTrainingPlanStateCopy(f.path);
+    assert.equal(report.ok, false); assert.equal(report.scopes, 2); assert.equal(report.affectedScopes, 2);
+    assert.equal(report.conflicts.length, 4);
+    assert.deepEqual(new Set(report.conflicts.map(c => c.schemaVersion)), new Set([1, 2]));
+    assert.deepEqual(new Set(report.conflicts.map(c => c.kind)), new Set(["locked_session_explicit_rest", "locked_session_week_plan_rest"]));
+    assert.ok(report.conflicts.every(c => c.localDate === date && c.revision > 0 && c.scope && c.source));
+    assert.ok(report.conflicts.every(c => c.lockedKind === "easy"));
+    assert.equal(JSON.stringify(report).includes("PRIVATE_PAYLOAD_SENTINEL"), false);
+    assert.equal(JSON.stringify(report).includes("distanceKm"), false);
+    assert.deepEqual(readFileSync(f.path), before);
+  } finally { f.close(); }
+});
+
+test("A-01 audit: malformed JSON, unsupported schema and invalid state cannot be reported as clean", () => {
+  const f = copyFixture();
+  try {
+    f.db.prepare("INSERT INTO mobile_training_state VALUES (?,1,?)").run(scope, '{"private":"PRIVATE_PAYLOAD_SENTINEL"');
+    f.insert("mobile_training_state_history", { schemaVersion: 99 }, 2);
+    f.insert("mobile_training_state_history", { schemaVersion: 2 }, 3);
+    const report = auditTrainingPlanStateCopy(f.path);
+    assert.equal(report.ok, false);
+    assert.equal(report.complete, false); assert.equal(report.affectedScopes, null, "an incomplete audit never claims zero affected scopes");
+    assert.deepEqual(report.issues.map(i => i.code).sort(), ["invalid_json", "invalid_state", "unsupported_state_schema"]);
+    assert.equal(JSON.stringify(report).includes("PRIVATE_PAYLOAD_SENTINEL"), false);
+  } finally { f.close(); }
+});
+
+test("A-01 audit: missing file and incompatible table schema are explicit failures and never create a DB", () => {
+  const f = copyFixture();
+  try {
+    const missing = join(f.root, "missing.sqlite");
+    assert.equal(auditTrainingPlanStateCopy(missing).issues[0]?.code, "control_copy_missing_or_not_file");
+    assert.equal(existsSync(missing), false);
+    f.db.exec("DROP TABLE mobile_training_state_history");
+    const report = auditTrainingPlanStateCopy(f.path);
+    assert.equal(report.ok, false); assert.equal(report.issues[0]?.code, "state_table_schema_mismatch");
+  } finally { f.close(); }
+});
+
+test("A-01 audit CLI: explicit copy path, no environment dependency, nonzero on conflicts or incomplete audit; no apply mode", () => {
+  const f = copyFixture();
+  try {
+    const health = join(f.root, "must-not-open.sqlite");
+    const invoke = (args: string[]) => spawnSync(process.execPath,
+      ["--import", "tsx", "scripts/audit-training-plan-state.mts", ...args],
+      { encoding: "utf8", env: { ...process.env, RAI_DATABASE: health, RAI_CONTROL_DATABASE: health } });
+    assert.equal(JSON.parse(invoke([]).stdout).issues[0].code, "control_db_copy_required");
+    f.insert("mobile_training_state", legacy());
+    const args = ["--control-db-copy", f.path];
+    assert.equal(invoke(args).status, 0);
+    f.insert("mobile_training_state_history", { ...fixture.payload2, lockedSession: lock });
+    const conflict = invoke(args);
+    assert.equal(conflict.status, 1); assert.equal(JSON.parse(conflict.stdout).affectedScopes, 1);
+    assert.equal(invoke([...args, "--apply"]).status, 1);
+    assert.equal(existsSync(health), false);
+  } finally { f.close(); }
+});

+    /// The candidate page and the current confirmed snapshot must describe the

     /// Newer state wins. A different plan instance is legitimate only with a
     /// strictly newer state revision; an old GET can never roll back a new write.
+    /// When the incoming response has no session view, it carries no instance
+    /// identity at all, so it may only keep or advance the revision — never
+    /// replace a newer snapshot just because the identity is unknown.
     static func acceptsSnapshot(incomingStateRevision: Int,
                                 incomingPlanInstanceId: String?,
                                 existingStateRevision: Int,

+        guard let incomingPlanInstanceId else {
+            return incomingStateRevision >= existingStateRevision
+        }
+        guard let existingPlanInstanceId else {
+            // First identity-bearing snapshot against an identity-less reference:
+            // equal revisions are not enough to prove it is the same plan.
+            return incomingStateRevision > existingStateRevision
+        }
+        if existingPlanInstanceId == incomingPlanInstanceId {
             return incomingStateRevision >= existingStateRevision
         }
         return incomingStateRevision > existingStateRevision
     }

+
+    static func durationText(_ value: Double?) -> String {
+        guard let value, value.isFinite, value >= 0 else { return "未知" }
+        guard value <= maximumDurationSeconds else { return "超过 7 天" }
+        guard let total = Int(exactly: value.rounded()) else { return "未知" }
         let hours = total / 3600, minutes = (total % 3600) / 60, seconds = total % 60
         if hours > 0 { return "\(hours)小时\(minutes)分" }
         if minutes > 0 { return seconds > 0 ? "\(minutes)分\(seconds)秒" : "\(minutes)分" }
diff --git a/apps/ios/RAI/TrainingPlanSessionEditorPolicy.swift b/apps/ios/RAI/TrainingPlanSessionEditorPolicy.swift
new file mode 100644
index 00000000..4879b95d
--- /dev/null
+++ b/apps/ios/RAI/TrainingPlanSessionEditorPolicy.swift
@@ -0,0 +1,185 @@
+import Foundation
+
+/// Pure draft rules for the native training-session editor.
+///
+/// The server owns dose measurement, including time-to-distance conversion.
+/// Only a distance-only structure has a locally knowable total. Persisted
+/// historical values survive unchanged edits; they are checked by the server.
+enum TrainingPlanSessionEditorPolicy {
+    struct DraftIdentity: Equatable {
+        let scope: PlanReceiptScope
+        let planInstanceId: String
+        let stateRevision: Int
+        let sessionId: String?
+        let sessionRevision: Int?
+
+        func issue(scope currentScope: PlanReceiptScope?, planInstanceId currentInstance: String?,
+                   stateRevision currentRevision: Int?, session: TrainingPlanSessionsView.Session?) -> String? {
+            guard currentScope == scope, currentInstance == planInstanceId, currentRevision == stateRevision,
+                  sessionId == session?.sessionId, sessionRevision == session?.sessionRevision,
+                  session == nil || session?.planInstanceId == planInstanceId else {
+                return "计划或课程修订已变化，旧草稿不能提交；请重新读取并重建草稿。"
+            }
+            if sessionId != nil && (session == nil || session?.lifecycle != "pending" || session?.supersededBySessionId != nil) {
+                return "当前课程不能修改。"
+            }
+            return nil
+        }
+
+        func issue(in snapshot: TrainingPlanStateResponseV2?) -> String? {
+            issue(scope: snapshot?.scope, planInstanceId: snapshot?.sessions?.planInstanceId,
+                stateRevision: snapshot?.stateRevision,
+                session: snapshot?.sessions?.sessions.first { $0.sessionId == sessionId })
+        }
+
+        func matches(_ draft: TrainingPlanSessionDraft) -> Bool {
+            switch draft {
+            case let .move(id, revision, _), let .edit(id, revision, _), let .cancel(id, revision), let .unlock(id, revision):
+                return id == sessionId && revision == sessionRevision
+            case .add, .setRest: return sessionId == nil
+            }
+        }
+    }
+
+    /// Mirrors `apps/api/src/session-structure.ts` (`MAX_STEP_METERS` /
+    /// `MAX_STEP_SECONDS`). The editor must not accept a step the server will
+    /// reject after preview.
+    static let maximumBlocks = 8
+    static let maximumStepsPerBlock = 8
+    static let maximumBlockIterations = 20
+    static let maximumStepMeters = 100_000.0
+    static let maximumStepSeconds = 4 * 60 * 60.0
+
+    enum StructureDistance: Equatable {
+        /// A valid structure with no distance goal: a purely time-based dose.
+        case none
+        /// Integer-meter total across warmup, every block iteration and cooldown.
+        case meters(Int)
+        /// Contains both time and distance goals. The sum of distance goals is
+        /// not its total; only the server has the pace model to measure it.
+        case mixed
+        /// Missing/legacy/open/invalid structure: not editable under the new
+        /// prescription contract until the user supplies a measurable dose.
+        case invalid
+    }
+
+    enum DistanceResolution: Equatable {
+        /// `nil` is an explicit wire `distanceKm: null`, not "leave unchanged".
+        case value(Double?)
+        /// Omit distanceKm: keep unchanged persisted dose or let the server
+        /// measure a changed/new structure. Never encode a fabricated zero.
+        case serverMeasured
+        case failure(String)
+    }
+
+    static func compatibleDistance(_ resolution: DistanceResolution, omissionSupported: Bool,
+                                   original: MobilePlannedDay?, structure: MobileSessionStructure?, kind: String) -> DistanceResolution {
+        guard resolution == .serverMeasured, !omissionSupported else { return resolution }
+        if let original, original.structure == structure, original.kind == kind {
+            return .value(original.distanceKm)
+        }
+        return .failure("服务器尚未确认省略距离能力，请升级服务器后再调整混合剂量课程。")
+    }
+
+    /// The editor starts new (or structure-less legacy) drafts with one blank
+    /// work step. It deliberately contains no distance or duration value, so a
+    /// user has to choose a real dose instead of accepting a fabricated one.
+    static func blankStructure() -> MobileSessionStructure {
+        .init(warmup: nil,
+              blocks: [.init(iterations: 1, steps: [.init(purpose: "work", goal: .open, alert: nil)])],
+              cooldown: nil)
+    }
+
+
+    /// Lossless display text for the editor's `distanceKm` field. The server
+    /// accepts whole meters (e.g. 8.05 km = 8,050 m); formatting with `%.1f`
+    /// would turn it into "8.1" and make a valid distance structure fail the
+    /// integer-meter consistency check. Whole kilometres omit the decimal part,
+    /// otherwise trailing zeros are trimmed from the three-digit meter remainder.
+    static func distanceText(_ value: Double?) -> String {
+        guard let value, value.isFinite, value >= 0 else { return "" }
+        guard let wholeMeters = Int(exactly: (value * 1_000).rounded()), wholeMeters >= 0 else { return "" }
+        let kilometres = wholeMeters / 1_000
+        let remainder = wholeMeters % 1_000
+        if remainder == 0 { return "\(kilometres)" }
+        var fraction = String(format: "%03d", remainder)
+        while fraction.hasSuffix("0") { fraction.removeLast() }
+        return "\(kilometres).\(fraction)"
+    }
+
+    static func structureDistance(_ structure: MobileSessionStructure?) -> StructureDistance {
+        guard let structure, !structure.blocks.isEmpty, structure.blocks.count <= maximumBlocks else { return .invalid }
+        var totalMeters = 0
+        var hasDistance = false
+        var hasTime = false
+
+        func validStep(_ step: MobileWorkoutStep, multiplier: Int) -> Bool {
+            switch step.goal {
+            case let .distance(meters):
+                guard meters.isFinite, meters >= 1, meters <= maximumStepMeters,
+                      let wholeMeters = Int(exactly: meters.rounded()) else { return false }
+                let added = wholeMeters.multipliedReportingOverflow(by: multiplier)
+                guard !added.overflow else { return false }
+                let total = totalMeters.addingReportingOverflow(added.partialValue)
+                guard !total.overflow else { return false }
+                totalMeters = total.partialValue
+                hasDistance = true
+                return true
+            case let .time(seconds):
+                hasTime = true
+                return seconds.isFinite && seconds >= 1 && seconds <= maximumStepSeconds && seconds.rounded() == seconds
+            case .open:
+                return false
+            }
+        }
+
+        if let warmup = structure.warmup, !validStep(warmup, multiplier: 1) { return .invalid }
+        for block in structure.blocks {
+            guard block.iterations >= 1, block.iterations <= maximumBlockIterations,
+                  !block.steps.isEmpty, block.steps.count <= maximumStepsPerBlock else { return .invalid }
+            for step in block.steps where !validStep(step, multiplier: block.iterations) { return .invalid }
+        }
+        if let cooldown = structure.cooldown, !validStep(cooldown, multiplier: 1) { return .invalid }
+        return hasDistance ? (hasTime ? .mixed : .meters(totalMeters)) : .none
+    }
+
+    /// Resolve the distance text against the structure that will be submitted.
+    ///
+    /// Pure time can send null; mixed doses delegate to the server. Unchanged
+    /// historical distance values are forwarded/preserved without local
+    /// rescaling. Explicit distance-only changes still compare whole metres.
+    static func resolveDistance(_ text: String, structure: MobileSessionStructure?,
+                                original: MobilePlannedDay? = nil, kind: String? = nil) -> DistanceResolution {
+        if let structure, structure.blocks.count > maximumBlocks ||
+            structure.blocks.contains(where: { $0.steps.count > maximumStepsPerBlock || $0.iterations > maximumBlockIterations }) {
+            return .failure("课程最多8个训练区块，每区块最多8步、20次重复；修改历史超限课程须先调整为合法结构。")
+        }
+        let unchanged = original != nil && original?.structure == structure && original?.kind == kind
+        switch structureDistance(structure) {
+        case .invalid:
+            return .failure("课程结构不完整或仍含未填写剂量；请填写明确的分段剂量。")
+        case .none:
+            if unchanged, original?.distanceKm != nil { return .serverMeasured }
+            return .value(nil)
+        case .mixed:
+            let trimmed = text.trimmingCharacters(in: .whitespacesAndNewlines)
+            if !trimmed.isEmpty {
+                guard let value = Double(trimmed), value.isFinite, value > 0, value <= 80 else {
+                    return .failure("总距离必须是大于 0 且不超过 80 公里的有效数值。")
+                }
+            }
+            return .serverMeasured
+        case let .meters(meters):
+            let trimmed = text.trimmingCharacters(in: .whitespacesAndNewlines)
+            guard let kilometers = Double(trimmed), kilometers.isFinite, kilometers > 0, kilometers <= 80,
+                  let displayedMeters = Int(exactly: (kilometers * 1_000).rounded()) else {
+                return .failure("有距离结构的课程必须填写总距离（公里）。")
+            }
+            if unchanged, kilometers == original?.distanceKm { return .value(kilometers) }
+            guard displayedMeters == meters else {
+                return .failure("总距离与分段结构不一致：结构合计 \(meters) 米，当前填写 \(displayedMeters) 米。")
+            }
+            return .value(Double(meters) / 1_000)
+        }
+    }
+}

+                planInstanceId: "plan", sessionId: "session", sessionRevision: 3,
+                executionRevision: 2, stateRevision: 7)?.contains("课程修订") == true &&
+              TrainingPlanExecutionPolicy.candidatePageMismatch(candidateResponse(executionRevision: 3),
+                planInstanceId: "plan", sessionId: "session", sessionRevision: 3,
+                executionRevision: 2, stateRevision: 7)?.contains("执行修订") == true,
+              "skip/confirm candidates must match the current state, session and execution revisions")
+        check(TrainingPlanExecutionPolicy.actionFailureMessage("confirm").contains("未能保存关联") &&
+              TrainingPlanExecutionPolicy.actionFailureMessage("retract").contains("未能撤回关联") &&
+              TrainingPlanExecutionPolicy.actionFailureMessage("skip").contains("未能跳过课程"),
+              "failed confirm/retract/skip actions always have user-visible copy")
         check(TrainingPlanExecutionPolicy.serverErrorMessage("training_execution_source_changed")?.contains("源训练记录已变化") == true &&
               TrainingPlanExecutionPolicy.serverErrorMessage("training_execution_link_conflict")?.contains("已关联其他课程") == true &&
               TrainingPlanExecutionPolicy.serverErrorMessage("training_execution_revision_conflict")?.contains("执行修订已变化") == true &&
               TrainingPlanExecutionPolicy.serverErrorMessage("unknown_code") == nil,
               "source-change and link-conflict errors have readable Chinese copy")
+        check(TrainingPlanExecutionPolicy.reasonTexts(["execution_retracted", "execution_source_changed"])
+                == ["已撤回关联", "源训练已更正"],
+              "retracted and corrected execution reason codes have Chinese mappings")
+        check(TrainingPlanExecutionPolicy.durationText(3_725) == "1小时2分" &&
+              TrainingPlanExecutionPolicy.durationText(59) == "59秒" &&
+              TrainingPlanExecutionPolicy.durationText(0) == "0秒",
+              "normal durations keep their existing readable format")
+        check(TrainingPlanExecutionPolicy.durationText(-1) == "未知" &&
+              TrainingPlanExecutionPolicy.durationText(.infinity) == "未知" &&
+              TrainingPlanExecutionPolicy.durationText(.nan) == "未知" &&
+              TrainingPlanExecutionPolicy.durationText(1e300) == "超过 7 天" &&
+              TrainingPlanExecutionPolicy.durationText(Double.greatestFiniteMagnitude) == "超过 7 天",
+              "an arbitrary finite Double never traps Int(value.rounded())")
+        let listSessions = [
+            session(lifecycle: "completed", date: "2026-10-26", id: "done"),
+            session(lifecycle: "cancelled", date: "2026-10-29", id: "cancelled"),
+            session(lifecycle: "pending", date: "2026-10-27", id: "past"),
+            session(lifecycle: "pending", date: "2026-10-28", id: "today"),
+            TrainingPlanSessionsView.Session(planInstanceId: "plan", sessionId: "old", sessionRevision: 1,
+                prescription: .init(localDate: "2026-10-25", kind: "easy", distanceKm: 5, structure: nil, reason: nil),
+                recommendation: nil, userOverride: nil, protection: "automatic", lifecycle: "pending",
+                availableAt: "2026-10-25T02:00:00.000Z", supersededBySessionId: "today")
+        ]
+        let visible = TrainingPlanExecutionPolicy.visibleSessions(listSessions)
+        check(visible.map(\.sessionId) == ["done", "past", "today", "cancelled"],
+              "finished and past sessions stay visible (only superseded identities are hidden), sorted by date")
+        check(TrainingPlanExecutionPolicy.sessionListState(session: listSessions[0], businessDate: "2026-10-28").isReadOnly &&
+              TrainingPlanExecutionPolicy.sessionListState(session: listSessions[1], businessDate: "2026-10-28").isReadOnly &&
+              TrainingPlanExecutionPolicy.sessionListState(session: listSessions[2], businessDate: "2026-10-28").isReadOnly &&
+              !TrainingPlanExecutionPolicy.sessionListState(session: listSessions[3], businessDate: "2026-10-28").isReadOnly,
+              "completed/cancelled/past rows are read-only while today's pending row remains editable")
+        check(TrainingPlanExecutionPolicy.sessionListState(session: listSessions[0], businessDate: "2026-10-28")
+                .caption.contains("查看记录") &&
+              !TrainingPlanExecutionPolicy.sessionListState(session: listSessions[0], businessDate: "2026-10-28")
+                .caption.contains("调整") &&
+              TrainingPlanExecutionPolicy.sessionListState(session: listSessions[2], businessDate: "2026-10-28")
+                .caption.contains("过去课程") &&
+              TrainingPlanExecutionPolicy.sessionListState(session: listSessions[3], businessDate: "2026-10-28")
+                .caption.contains("点按查看"),
+              "read-only rows say 查看记录 and never promise 调整; an editable automatic row stays a view action")
+        let protectedToday = TrainingPlanSessionsView.Session(planInstanceId: "plan", sessionId: "protected",
+            sessionRevision: 1,
+            prescription: .init(localDate: "2026-10-28", kind: "easy", distanceKm: 5, structure: nil, reason: nil),
+            recommendation: nil, userOverride: nil, protection: "user_protected", lifecycle: "pending",
+            availableAt: "2026-10-25T02:00:00.000Z", supersededBySessionId: nil)
+        check(TrainingPlanExecutionPolicy.sessionListState(session: protectedToday, businessDate: "2026-10-28")
+                .caption.contains("查看/调整"),
+              "an editable user-protected row keeps the adjust action")
         let history = TrainingPlanExecutionPolicy.historyRows(
             sessions: [session(lifecycle: "completed", id: "session-a"),
                        session(lifecycle: "pending", date: "2026-10-29", id: "session-b")],
diff --git a/apps/ios/tests/TrainingPlanSessionEditorRegression.swift b/apps/ios/tests/TrainingPlanSessionEditorRegression.swift
new file mode 100644
index 00000000..b9e91286
--- /dev/null
+++ b/apps/ios/tests/TrainingPlanSessionEditorRegression.swift
@@ -0,0 +1,247 @@
+import Foundation
+
+/// P2.10 pure regression: the session editor may send `distanceKm: null` for a
+/// time-only structure, must not fabricate a 5 km dose, and delegates mixed
+/// structure measurement to the server while preserving historical totals.
+@main struct TrainingPlanSessionEditorRegression {
+    static func main() throws {
+        var count = 0
+        func check(_ condition: @autoclosure () -> Bool, _ name: String) {
+            precondition(condition(), name)
+            count += 1
+            print("PASS: \(name)")
+        }
+        func time(_ seconds: Double) -> MobileWorkoutStep {
+            .init(purpose: "work", goal: .time(seconds: seconds), alert: nil)
+        }
+        func distance(_ meters: Double) -> MobileWorkoutStep {
+            .init(purpose: "work", goal: .distance(meters: meters), alert: nil)
+        }
+        func structure(warmup: MobileWorkoutStep? = nil,
+                       blocks: [MobileIntervalBlock],
+                       cooldown: MobileWorkoutStep? = nil) -> MobileSessionStructure {
+            .init(warmup: warmup, blocks: blocks, cooldown: cooldown)
+        }
+
+        // EDIT-01: the identity is captured when the form opens, before refresh.
+        struct SessionFixture: Decodable {
+            let scope: PlanReceiptScope
+            let initial: TrainingPlanSessionsView.Session
+        }
+        let sessionFixture = try JSONDecoder().decode(SessionFixture.self, from: Data(contentsOf:
+            URL(fileURLWithPath: "apps/ios/tests/fixtures/training-plan-sessions-v2.json")))
+        let opened = sessionFixture.initial
+        let binding = TrainingPlanSessionEditorPolicy.DraftIdentity(scope: sessionFixture.scope,
+            planInstanceId: opened.planInstanceId, stateRevision: 7,
+            sessionId: opened.sessionId, sessionRevision: opened.sessionRevision)
+        check(binding.issue(scope: sessionFixture.scope, planInstanceId: opened.planInstanceId,
+            stateRevision: 7, session: opened) == nil, "EDIT-01: original form identity is usable")
+        let refreshed = TrainingPlanSessionsView.Session(planInstanceId: opened.planInstanceId,
+            sessionId: opened.sessionId, sessionRevision: opened.sessionRevision + 1,
+            prescription: opened.prescription, recommendation: opened.recommendation, userOverride: opened.userOverride,
+            protection: "user_protected", lifecycle: "pending", availableAt: opened.availableAt, supersededBySessionId: nil)
+        check(binding.issue(scope: sessionFixture.scope, planInstanceId: opened.planInstanceId,
+            stateRevision: 8, session: refreshed) != nil,
+              "EDIT-01: background refresh cannot wrap an old draft with the new revision")
+        check(binding.issue(scope: sessionFixture.scope, planInstanceId: "replacement", stateRevision: 7, session: opened) != nil,
+              "EDIT-01: replacing the plan invalidates the open form")
+        check(binding.issue(scope: sessionFixture.scope, planInstanceId: opened.planInstanceId,
+            stateRevision: 8, session: opened) != nil, "EDIT-01: even unchanged course needs explicit reload after state change")
+        let addBinding = TrainingPlanSessionEditorPolicy.DraftIdentity(scope: sessionFixture.scope,
+            planInstanceId: opened.planInstanceId, stateRevision: 7, sessionId: nil, sessionRevision: nil)
+        check(addBinding.issue(scope: sessionFixture.scope, planInstanceId: "replacement", stateRevision: 8, session: nil) != nil,
+              "EDIT-01: new course drafts cannot cross plan instances")
+
+        // A purely time-based prescription (the contract permits distanceKm:null).
+        let timeOnly = structure(warmup: time(600),
+            blocks: [.init(iterations: 3, steps: [time(300), time(60)])],
+            cooldown: time(300))
+        check(TrainingPlanSessionEditorPolicy.structureDistance(timeOnly) == .none,
+              "a structure with no distance goal is recognised as purely time-based")
+        check(TrainingPlanSessionEditorPolicy.resolveDistance("", structure: timeOnly) == .value(nil),
+              "an empty distance on a time-only structure encodes distanceKm:null")
+        check(TrainingPlanSessionEditorPolicy.resolveDistance("5.0", structure: timeOnly) == .value(nil),
+              "a stale display distance cannot turn a time-only structure into a fake 5 km prescription")
+
+        // A distance structure requires an exactly matching displayed total.
+        let distanceOnly = structure(blocks: [.init(iterations: 2, steps: [distance(5_000)])])
+        check(TrainingPlanSessionEditorPolicy.structureDistance(distanceOnly) == .meters(10_000),
+              "block iterations multiply the structure's integer-meter distance")
+        check(TrainingPlanSessionEditorPolicy.resolveDistance("10", structure: distanceOnly) == .value(10),
+              "a consistent total distance is accepted and normalised from the structure")
+        check(TrainingPlanSessionEditorPolicy.resolveDistance("10.0004", structure: distanceOnly) == .value(10),
+              "integer-meter comparison tolerates floating display noise, not a real mismatch")
+        if case .failure = TrainingPlanSessionEditorPolicy.resolveDistance("9.999", structure: distanceOnly) {
+            check(true, "a displayed total below the structure's metres is rejected")
+        } else {
+            check(false, "a displayed total below the structure's metres is rejected")
+        }
+        if case .failure = TrainingPlanSessionEditorPolicy.resolveDistance("", structure: distanceOnly) {
+            check(true, "a distance structure cannot be saved with an empty distance")
+        } else {
+            check(false, "a distance structure cannot be saved with an empty distance")
+        }
+        if case .failure = TrainingPlanSessionEditorPolicy.resolveDistance("ten", structure: distanceOnly) {
+            check(true, "a non-numeric distance is rejected instead of silently becoming zero")
+        } else {
+            check(false, "a non-numeric distance is rejected instead of silently becoming zero")
+        }
+
+        // Mixed and edge structures.
+        let mixed = structure(warmup: distance(1_000),
+            blocks: [.init(iterations: 4, steps: [distance(800), time(90)])],
+            cooldown: time(300))
+        var negotiated = RaiHandshake(serverId: "server", athleteId: "athlete", datasetEpoch: "epoch",
+            syncProtocolVersion: 1, apiVersion: "v1", trainingPlanContractVersion: 2,
+            trainingPlanSessionContractVersion: 1, trainingPlanSessionCommandContractVersion: 1,
+            planPublicationContractVersion: 1)
+        for version in [nil, 0, 2, -1] as [Int?] {
+            negotiated.trainingPlanSessionDistanceOmissionContractVersion = version
+            check(!negotiated.trainingPlanSessionDistanceOmissionSupported,
+                  "COMPAT-01: absent/unknown omission capability does not inherit session command 1")
+        }
+        negotiated.trainingPlanSessionDistanceOmissionContractVersion = 1
+        check(negotiated.trainingPlanSessionDistanceOmissionSupported, "COMPAT-01: exact omission 1 is supported")
+        negotiated.planPublicationContractVersion = nil
+        check(!negotiated.trainingPlanSessionDistanceOmissionSupported, "COMPAT-01: omission does not bypass durable/session dependencies")
+        let oldMixed = MobilePlannedDay(localDate: "2026-10-28", kind: "easy", distanceKm: 5.8, structure: mixed, reason: nil)
+        check(TrainingPlanSessionEditorPolicy.compatibleDistance(.serverMeasured, omissionSupported: false,
+            original: oldMixed, structure: mixed, kind: "easy") == .value(5.8),
+              "COMPAT-01: unchanged historical mixed course keeps explicit legal old distance")
+        if case .failure = TrainingPlanSessionEditorPolicy.compatibleDistance(.serverMeasured, omissionSupported: false,
+            original: nil, structure: mixed, kind: "easy") {
+            check(true, "COMPAT-01: new mixed dose requires upgrade before sending omission")
+        } else { check(false, "old server cannot receive unnegotiated omission") }
+        check(TrainingPlanSessionEditorPolicy.compatibleDistance(.value(nil), omissionSupported: false,
+            original: nil, structure: timeOnly, kind: "easy") == .value(nil), "COMPAT-01: explicit time-only null stays compatible")
+        let delegated = TrainingPlanSessionDraft.add(oldMixed, deriveDistance: true)
+        check(delegated.requiresDistanceOmission, "COMPAT-01: omitted add requires capability")
+        struct WireCommand: Encodable { let draft: TrainingPlanSessionDraft }
+        let delegatedWire = try JSONEncoder().encode(WireCommand(draft: delegated))
+        check(TrainingPlanSessionDraft.payloadRequiresDistanceOmission(delegatedWire),
+              "COMPAT-01: durable omitted add cannot replay to a downgraded server")
+        let reasonOnly = TrainingPlanSessionDraft.edit(sessionId: "s", sessionRevision: 1,
+            patch: .init(reason: "legacy legal patch", updatesReason: true))
+        let reasonWire = try JSONEncoder().encode(WireCommand(draft: reasonOnly))
+        check(!reasonOnly.requiresDistanceOmission &&
+              !TrainingPlanSessionDraft.payloadRequiresDistanceOmission(reasonWire),
+              "COMPAT-01: old reason-only patches remain compatible without new capability")
+        check(TrainingPlanSessionEditorPolicy.structureDistance(mixed) == .mixed,
+              "mixed dose is not mislabelled as its 4,200 m distance-step subtotal")
+        check(TrainingPlanSessionEditorPolicy.resolveDistance("5.822", structure: mixed) == .serverMeasured,
+              "D-02: a server-measured mixed total can reach preview")
+        check(TrainingPlanSessionEditorPolicy.resolveDistance("", structure: mixed) == .serverMeasured,
+              "a mixed draft can ask the server to measure without inventing a total")
+        for invalid in ["0", "-1", "NaN", "infinity", "abc", "80.001"] {
+            if case .failure = TrainingPlanSessionEditorPolicy.resolveDistance(invalid, structure: mixed) {
+                check(true, "mixed draft rejects invalid explicit distance: \(invalid)")
+            } else { check(false, "invalid mixed distance cannot become zero or reach preview") }
+        }
+        check(TrainingPlanSessionEditorPolicy.structureDistance(nil) == .invalid,
+              "a legacy structure-less prescription is not misread as time-only")
+        let blank = TrainingPlanSessionEditorPolicy.blankStructure()
+        check(TrainingPlanSessionEditorPolicy.structureDistance(blank) == .invalid &&
+              blank.blocks.count == 1 && blank.blocks[0].steps.count == 1 &&
+              blank.blocks[0].steps[0].goal == .open,
+              "the editor starts without a fabricated 5 km dose; the blank open step must be filled by the user")
+        if case .failure = TrainingPlanSessionEditorPolicy.resolveDistance("5", structure: blank) {
+            check(true, "the blank/open structure cannot silently become a 5 km prescription")
+        } else {
+            check(false, "the blank/open structure cannot silently become a 5 km prescription")
+        }
+        let openLegacy = structure(blocks: [.init(iterations: 1, steps: [.init(purpose: "work", goal: .open, alert: nil)])])
+        check(TrainingPlanSessionEditorPolicy.structureDistance(openLegacy) == .invalid,
+              "legacy open steps stay readable but cannot back a new editable dose")
+
+        // Whole-meter display values must round-trip losslessly. A 8.05 km
+        // prescription is 8,050 m; %.1f ("8.1") would fail its own structure.
+        let eightPointZeroFive = structure(blocks: [.init(iterations: 1, steps: [distance(8_050)])])
+        let exactText = TrainingPlanSessionEditorPolicy.distanceText(8.05)
+        check(exactText == "8.05" &&
+              TrainingPlanSessionEditorPolicy.resolveDistance(exactText, structure: eightPointZeroFive) == .value(8.05),
+              "the editor seeds 8.05 km losslessly, and the seeded text matches the 8,050 m structure")
+        check(TrainingPlanSessionEditorPolicy.distanceText(8) == "8",
+              "whole kilometres omit the decimal part")
+        let eightThousand = structure(blocks: [.init(iterations: 1, steps: [distance(8_000)])])
+        check(TrainingPlanSessionEditorPolicy.resolveDistance(
+                TrainingPlanSessionEditorPolicy.distanceText(8), structure: eightThousand) == .value(8),
+              "a whole-kilometre display value resolves against its 8,000 m structure")
+        check(TrainingPlanSessionEditorPolicy.distanceText(8.001) == "8.001" &&
+              TrainingPlanSessionEditorPolicy.distanceText(nil) == "" &&
+              TrainingPlanSessionEditorPolicy.distanceText(-1) == "",
+              "distance text keeps the minimal exact fraction and treats absent/invalid values as empty")
+        check(TrainingPlanSessionEditorPolicy.structureDistance(
+                structure(blocks: [.init(iterations: 1, steps: [distance(100_000)])])) == .meters(100_000) &&
+              TrainingPlanSessionEditorPolicy.structureDistance(
+                structure(blocks: [.init(iterations: 1, steps: [distance(100_001)])])) == .invalid,
+              "a single step is capped at the server's MAX_STEP_METERS=100000")
+
+        let eightSteps = structure(blocks: [.init(iterations: 1, steps: Array(repeating: distance(100), count: 8))])
+        let nineSteps = structure(blocks: [.init(iterations: 1, steps: Array(repeating: distance(100), count: 9))])
+        check(TrainingPlanSessionEditorPolicy.structureDistance(eightSteps) == .meters(800), "D-03: eight steps are editable")
+        check(TrainingPlanSessionEditorPolicy.structureDistance(nineSteps) == .invalid, "D-03: nine steps cannot reach preview")
+        check(TrainingPlanSessionEditorPolicy.structureDistance(structure(blocks:
+            Array(repeating: .init(iterations: 1, steps: [distance(100)]), count: 8))) == .meters(800), "D-03: eight blocks are editable")
+        check(TrainingPlanSessionEditorPolicy.structureDistance(structure(blocks:
+            Array(repeating: .init(iterations: 1, steps: [distance(100)]), count: 9))) == .invalid, "D-03: nine blocks cannot reach preview")
+        check(TrainingPlanSessionEditorPolicy.structureDistance(structure(blocks: [.init(iterations: 20, steps: [distance(100)])])) == .meters(2000), "D-03: twenty iterations are editable")
+        check(TrainingPlanSessionEditorPolicy.structureDistance(structure(blocks: [.init(iterations: 21, steps: [distance(100)])])) == .invalid, "D-03: twenty-one iterations cannot reach preview")
+        let historical = MobilePlannedDay(localDate: "2026-10-28", kind: "easy", distanceKm: 0.9, structure: nineSteps, reason: nil)
+        if case .failure = TrainingPlanSessionEditorPolicy.resolveDistance("0.9", structure: nineSteps, original: historical, kind: "easy") {
+            check(true, "D-03: an unchanged historical over-limit structure cannot be resubmitted as content")
+        } else { check(false, "D-03: history is not a write-validation exemption") }
+        let decoded = try JSONDecoder().decode(MobilePlannedDay.self, from: JSONEncoder().encode(historical))
+        check(decoded.structure == nineSteps, "D-03: history decoding preserves every step")
+
+        struct DistanceFixture: Decodable {
+            struct Course: Decodable {
+                struct Measurement: Decodable { let distanceKm: Double; let durationSeconds: Double }
+                let id: String
+                let prescription: MobilePlannedDay
+                let measured: Measurement
+                let editorDistanceMode: String
+            }
+            let cases: [Course]
+        }
+        let fixture = try JSONDecoder().decode(DistanceFixture.self, from: Data(contentsOf:
+            URL(fileURLWithPath: "apps/ios/tests/fixtures/training-session-distance-v1.json")))
+        for item in fixture.cases {
+            let day = item.prescription
+            let text = TrainingPlanSessionEditorPolicy.distanceText(day.distanceKm)
+            let result = TrainingPlanSessionEditorPolicy.resolveDistance(text, structure: day.structure, original: day, kind: day.kind)
+            let updatesDistance: Bool, distanceKm: Double?
+            switch result {
+            case let .value(value): updatesDistance = true; distanceKm = value
+            case .serverMeasured: updatesDistance = false; distanceKm = nil
+            case .failure: preconditionFailure("fixed legal course rejected: \(item.id)")
+            }
+            check(updatesDistance == (item.editorDistanceMode != "omit"), "shared \(item.id): editor measurement authority matches server contract")
+            if updatesDistance {
+                check(distanceKm == day.distanceKm, "shared \(item.id): persisted total is not rounded or invented")
+            }
+            let patch = TrainingPlanSessionPatch(kind: day.kind, distanceKm: distanceKm, updatesDistance: updatesDistance,
+                structure: day.structure, reason: day.reason, updatesReason: true)
+            let wire = try JSONSerialization.jsonObject(with: JSONEncoder().encode(patch)) as! [String: Any]
+            check((wire["distanceKm"] != nil) == updatesDistance, "shared \(item.id): patch encodes omission distinctly from null")
+            if item.editorDistanceMode == "null" { check(wire["distanceKm"] is NSNull, "pure time has explicit null distance") }
+            let add = TrainingPlanSessionDraft.add(day, deriveDistance: !updatesDistance)
+            let addWire = try JSONSerialization.jsonObject(with: JSONEncoder().encode(add)) as! [String: Any]
+            check(((addWire["prescription"] as! [String: Any])["distanceKm"] != nil) == updatesDistance,
+                  "shared \(item.id): add can delegate to the server without encoding a fake null/zero distance")
+            if item.id == "mixed" {
+                check(item.measured.distanceKm > 4.2 && item.measured.durationSeconds > 0,
+                      "shared mixed baseline includes time-step distance; native code does not reproduce its pace model")
+            }
+            if item.id == "historical-distance-only" {
+                let original = day.structure!
+                let changed = MobileSessionStructure(warmup: original.warmup,
+                    blocks: original.blocks.map { .init(iterations: $0.iterations + 1, steps: $0.steps) },
+                    cooldown: original.cooldown)
+                if case .failure = TrainingPlanSessionEditorPolicy.resolveDistance(text, structure: changed, original: day, kind: day.kind) {
+                    check(true, "changed distance structure cannot reuse historical rounded total")
+                } else { check(false, "changed distance structure must revalidate") }
+            }
+        }
+        print("TrainingPlanSessionEditor regression: \(count) checks passed")
+    }
+}

diff --git a/scripts/audit-training-plan-state.mts b/scripts/audit-training-plan-state.mts
new file mode 100644
index 00000000..ed17622f
--- /dev/null
+++ b/scripts/audit-training-plan-state.mts
@@ -0,0 +1,20 @@
+import { parseArgs } from "node:util";
+import { resolve } from "node:path";
+import { auditTrainingPlanStateCopy } from "../apps/api/src/training-plan-state-audit.js";
+
+try {
+  const { values } = parseArgs({ options: { "control-db-copy": { type: "string" }, help: { type: "boolean" } }, strict: true });
+  if (values.help) {
+    process.stdout.write("Read-only A-01 audit of an independent, consistent SQLite control database copy.\nUsage: node --import tsx scripts/audit-training-plan-state.mts --control-db-copy /absolute/offline/control.sqlite\nExit 0: no conflicts/issues; 1: conflicts or incomplete audit. No apply mode.\n");
+  } else if (!values["control-db-copy"]?.trim()) {
+    process.stdout.write(`${JSON.stringify({ ok: false, issues: [{ code: "control_db_copy_required" }] })}\n`);
+    process.exitCode = 1;
+  } else {
+    const report = auditTrainingPlanStateCopy(resolve(values["control-db-copy"]));
+    process.stdout.write(`${JSON.stringify(report, null, 2)}\n`);
+    process.exitCode = report.ok ? 0 : 1;
+  }
+} catch {
+  process.stdout.write(`${JSON.stringify({ ok: false, issues: [{ code: "invalid_arguments" }] })}\n`);
+  process.exitCode = 1;
+}
